Three canonical axes: with R_i = rho(A_(i4)), a = 1/(1+t), b = t/(1+t), Q_i = I - P_i, the overlap matrix tr(P_i P_j) has 1 on the diagonal, a above and b below; for W = R_1 R_2^-1 R_1^-1 R_3 the all-maximal channel t^4 tr(P_1 Q_2 Q_1 P_3) = -t^4 a^3 b and the channel changing only the third choice t^2 tr(P_1 Q_2 P_1 P_3) = t^2 a b (1 - a b) both have degree 1 and sum to t^2 a^2 b of degree 0: strict all-maximal-channel dominance is FALSE for three canonical axes. W is not a kernel element (W = I would make v_3 proportional to R_1 v_2 = v_2 + (t - 1) v_1, against independence) and not Brunnian, so this refutes only the unrestricted product proof, not BBB-MULTISET or a Brunnian-specific repair; regrouping as conjugated reflections works but arbitrary prefixes leave the old-B_3 orbit, and no general column-stabilizer argument exists. The main gap is unchanged: equal anchored coloured profiles must force the simultaneous localization of BBB-LOCAL, or a replacement that detects returns through full support. Claude's read: the degree bounds (-1, gap 2 versus gain 1), the det-1 normalization, and the degree count of the two channels check; the exact channel identities are Astra's with an internal read.

Codex 170958 (17:12 wall clock), BBB-PERRON (TRUSTED; read by Claude): a Perron-eigenline mechanism for a substantial restricted class, with an explicit Brunnian BI_4 family detected at every real t > 2. Let x = A_14, y = A_24, z = A_34 be the canonical free generators of K_4 = ker(P_4 -> P_3), H a nonempty positive word with primitive positive root H_0, m nonzero. If g = P Q^-1 or Q^-1 P for positive words P, Q (empty allowed), then at every real t > 2, [rho_t(g), rho_t(H)^m] = I iff g lies in <H_0> as a free-group word. Mechanism: with the canonical axis vectors v_i and covectors l_i (l_i(v_j) = t/(1+t) for j < i, 1/(1+t) for j > i), R_i = rho(A_(i4)) = I + (t^2 - 1) v_i l_i changes only coordinate i in the v-basis, to t^2 c_i + t(t-1) sum_(j<i) c_j + (t-1) sum_(j>i) c_j (checked by Claude), so for t > 2 it sends every nonzero nonnegative vector into the region where coordinate i exceeds the sum of the others (new_i - sum_(j != i) c_j >= t^2 c_i + (t-2) sum_(j != i) c_j > 0); these regions are disjoint, so the vector cone decodes the first letter of a positive word and (by the transposed rule) the covector cone decodes the last; positive words preserve the cone, so equal-length positive words with proportional images of one nonnegative vector are literally equal. A positive word using all letters of its support is a strictly positive matrix there, so Perron-Frobenius gives a simple nonnegative dual eigenline l_H with eigenvalue lambda > 1 (unused coordinates eigenvalue 1), and lambda^m stays simple for m < 0. If g = P Q^-1 commutes with H^m it preserves that line, so l_H P is proportional to l_H Q; last-letter decoding cancels the common suffix, leaving g = T^(+-1) with T positive and l_H T proportional to l_H; then l_H H T and l_H T H are proportional (both to l_H), of equal length, so HT = TH literally and the commuting-words lemma gives T = H_0^s. The Q^-1 P case uses the primal Perron line. No infinite-word convergence and no four-strand faithfulness premise. Family: delta = xyz = Delta_4^2 Delta_3^-2; P_k = (xyz)^(2k) yx, Q_k = yx (zxy)^(2k), g_k = P_k Q_k^-1 = [delta^(2k), [y, x]] (checked by Claude: (xy)^-1 (xyz)(xy) = zxy, so Q_k = yx (xy)^-1 delta^(2k) (xy) and g_k = [delta^(2k), (yx)(xy)^-1] with (yx)(xy)^-1 = [y, x]), W_(k,m) = [g_k, z^m]. Every W_(k,m) is Brunnian (deleting x or y makes P_k = Q_k by (ab)^n a = a (ba)^n; deleting z kills the outer commutator; deleting strand 4 kills the point-pushing word), and rho_4(Delta_3^4)(-1) = I makes rho_4(delta^(2k))(-1) = rho_4(Delta_4^(4k))(-1) central, so g_k and W_(k,m) specialize to I at t = -1 (centrality, not scalarity, is what is used). P_k and Q_k start with different letters and g_k has zero z-exponent sum, so g_k is not a z-power, and the theorem with H = z gives rho_t(W_(k,m)) != I for every real t > 2. Calibration k = m = 1 at t = -1 and t = 3 (Codex). This placement of the nested commutators differs from BBB-RETURN and from Phi_0; no identification and no Gamma certificate. Remaining gap: for a general four-run P Q^-1 R S^-1 both middle branches can leave the positive cone and no distinguished eigenline is supplied; products of detected commutators are not automatically detected; so this is an exact family theorem in the held slice, not a reduction of all Brunnian words to it. Prior art noted: Witzel-Zaremsky (Euclidean buildings, different generator pair). BBB-MULTISET OPEN, holder Codex; the scalar probe and profile-to-localization remain unproved; Claude's Gamma construction still running.

Codex 172527 (17:26 wall clock), extensions of BBB-PERRON tested and refuted; a parked finite certificate. (1) Positive re-entry is insufficient: w = (xyz y^-1)^2 has four cyclic sign runs and is not a positive fraction, yet its Burau matrix is strictly positive for every t > 1 (rho(xyz) = t I + t(t-1)(t^2, t, 1)^T (1,1,1), and the one doubtful row of rho(xyz y^-1), ((t-1)^2, t - 1 + t^-1, (t-1)^2 (t^2+1)/t), is positive); not Brunnian; refutes only the unrestricted inference. (2) Four-run Brunnian words can have arbitrarily large commutator length in K = ker(P_4 -> P_3): B_n = (xy)^n (yx)^-n (zyx)^n (zxy)^-n is Brunnian, cyclically reduced with four cyclic sign runs; with q = #(xyz subwords) - #(z^-1 y^-1 x^-1 subwords) in the reduced word, seam accounting gives |q| <= 18 for a commutator and |q| <= 24r - 6 for a product of r commutators, while q(B_n) = -(n-1), so cl_K(B_n) >= ceil((n+5)/24) and B_20 is not a single commutator in K even up to conjugacy and inversion; so "four runs => [positive fraction, positive word]" fails substantially (no claim that B_n lies in BI_4; a classification using the extra t = -1 condition untested). (3) Finite cone certificate (OPEN/PARKED, IDEAS): in the same basis C = [[0,0,t^-1],[1,0,0],[0,1,0]] and R = R_1 = [[t^2, t-1, t-1],[0,1,0],[0,0,1]] satisfy C^3 = t^-1 I and C R_i C^-1 = R_(i+1); RC is the companion matrix of (s - t)(s^2 + s + 1), C the companion of s^3 - t^-1, their difference of rank one (a calibration of the Burau/hypergeometric connection). Projectively <R, C> is Z * C_3 with the canonical R_1, R_2, R_3 generating its index-three free subgroup; projective injectivity on that subgroup is equivalent to injectivity on Z * C_3, and generic scalar kernels on the free subgroup are excluded by differentiating at t = 1 (exponent sums) plus the determinant. A single rational specialization t_0 > 2 with nonempty projective domains X_+, X_-, X = X_+ u X_-, Y = C X u C^2 X satisfying X cap Y empty, R Y and R X_+ inside X_+, R^-1 Y and R^-1 X_- inside X_- would give full free-product ping-pong, hence (with the Long/Brunnian reduction) generic Burau_4 faithfulness; finite unions of rational polyhedral cones would make the checks exact and finite. No domains supplied; related finite cone constructions for a different hypergeometric group (Frieden-Gelinas-Soucy arXiv:2111.14692) do not automatically apply. Would still not prove T. BBB-MULTISET OPEN and held.

Codex 173602 (17:39 wall clock), BBB-CONE (TRUSTED, recorded): the finite semialgebraic cone certificate of 17:25 is impossible at every rational t > 2: with R = [[t^2, t-1, t-1],[0,1,0],[0,0,1]] and C = [[0,0,t^-1],[1,0,0],[0,1,0]] there is no nonempty open semialgebraic U in RP^2 with R^-1 U, R^-1 C U inside U and U disjoint from C U, because A_n = R^-n C has, for large n, a dominant complex pair whose real invariant line L_n carries an irrational rotation (the eigenvalue ratio tends to zeta with zeta + 1/zeta = 1/(t+1) - 2, not a root of unity), the L_n are pairwise distinct, forward invariance forces each L_n into the closure of U and then either into U (contradiction with C L_n in the disjoint C U) or into the boundary of U, which, being contained in the zero set of one polynomial, cannot contain infinitely many lines. Covers domains with nonempty interior; nothing about lower-dimensional or nonalgebraic domains. The cone implementation is REFUTED; BBB-PERRON survives; the Burau_4 target itself is now superseded by Caleb's redirect below.

### V. CALEB'S REDIRECT (17:39 wall clock): build a counterexample, intelligently, with Astra

Caleb, 17:3x: "Why don't we redirect to trying to find a counterexample? we've developed enough and learned enough to intelligently build cases now, which is something I want you to do with Astra." Decision recorded: the proof program (Lemma Q, the split, the Burau_4 repair, the genus-two faithfulness rung, the family theorems) stops as a target; everything banked stays banked and becomes the design constraint set. Roles: Codex designs candidates with reasons; Claude certifies or refutes each candidate by exact computation (one exact bracket, one exact identity, one exact invariant per candidate: decisive by construction, not a sweep). The BBB gap note stays banked for Caleb's decision about the authors.
Checklist a counterexample K must satisfy (all from the board): nontrivial knot with V_K = 1; det 1, Arf 0, H_1(Sigma_2(K); Z/3) = 0; a_2(K) = 0 and every Jones-type finite-type invariant zero; span 0, so no diagram is adequate at either end with nonzero potential degree (not alternating, positive, Montesinos, closed 3-braid, semiadequate: Stoimenow); at least 25 crossings (Tuzun-Sikora through 24); Khr(K; Q) chi-trivial with the Lee survivor at (0, s), every column balanced; not a torus knot; the braid-index and TB conditions are NOT available (false bound); for Sakuma-type constructions the quotient knot has V = 1 in F_16 and a_2 = 0 (FROB5, U5-A2SUB).
Construction routes with exact certificates: C1 constant-sum twist templates (the only known mechanism producing positive-dimensional Jones fibres of distinct hyperbolic knots: Kanenobu; in board terms two crossing-circle sites with vanishing mixed sectors b = c = 0, i.e. turning back at either site makes the other nugatory, and the unit line condition a + d A^(8m) = 1 from D-MULTITWIST; D-TWIST-HYP then gives infinitely many counterexamples when the active exterior is hyperbolic; Kanenobu's own template has det 25 (K(0,0) = 4_1 # 4_1) so it cannot be unit, and the design question is which band and seed configurations have the mutual-nugatory property and what (a, d) they produce); C2 Sakuma links (a nonsplit c u U with V = V(U_2) gives a flat Jones-unit twist family of the unknot, nontrivial for n != 0 unless c is nugatory; FROB5 residual class, pilot C(2,-4n,2,2n) with 5 | n; one exact bracket of the Sakuma link per candidate); C3 a genus-two kernel element (Theorem B certificate R_f(A) R_f(1/A) = Delta_2^2; constraints G2-ORTH, G2-PAIR-COMM, G2-ZRHO-SPAN; Ito/plats turn it into 3-bridge knots with V = 1); C4 an EKT-type tangle substitution closing to a knot (wrapping >= 2 pattern, det J = g^2 with g odd, U5-W2-Q). Priority C1, then C2.

Claude's explicit Gamma check, final report (17:44 wall clock; scratchpad bbb-attack/, scripts, traces and JSON results there; one agent, 467k tokens, 114 minutes): UNDECIDED, construction infeasible at the size of the specified braid; exact partial facts banked. Conventions fixed and validated against the paper's own Section 7 example term by term (sigma_i counterclockwise half twist, words acting on arcs left to right, Moody exponent +1 per clockwise pass, Fox-Jacobian Burau with the arc class in row 3, <loop, alpha-tilde> = (1 - t^-1) M_arc checked on 600 random braids); the paper's figure 7.4 lists two terms of M_Gamma as t^-2, t^0 where the figure and the push braid give t^-4, t^-2 (a harmless slip; |M(-1)| = 20 either way). y_1 = sigma_3^2, y_2 = sigma_3 sigma_2^2 sigma_3, y_3 = sigma_3 sigma_2 sigma_1^2 sigma_2 sigma_3 (Fig. 6.13); Gamma_1 (Fig. 6.3, raster-traced) = y_2^-1 y_1^-1 y_2^2 y_3^-1 y_2^-1 y_1 y_2, a conjugate of y_3^-1, beginning with y_2^-1 as the paper says; Phi_0's y-word (30 letters) starts and ends with y_3^-1, so m = 0 already gives a freely reduced proper product and the y_1^m conjugation is unnecessary. Sizes (exact Burau coefficient sums as a proxy, with the actual intersection number where computed): Phi_0: 1.26 x 10^7 (row 3: 4.26 x 10^6; actual iota = 976,672, cutting sequence 6.8 million, 59 s); the conjugated Phi with m = 31 (braid length 300): 1.55 x 10^11 (row 3: 7.67 x 10^10), so the arc has intersection number of order 10^10 and the push loop of order iota^2: far beyond explicit cutting sequences (stop rule). Smallest Brunnian-cap-BI_4 element found: [(sigma_2 sigma_3)^6, [A_14, [A_24, A_34]]] with coefficient sum 100,802, but every proper-product conjugate Gamma_1^-1 y_1^m (.) y_1^-m Gamma_1 (m = 0, 1, 2) has coefficient sum >= 7.9 x 10^6 (iota about 10^6), above the stop rule. Facts for Phi_0 itself: rho_4 and rho_5 identity at t = -1, not at t = 2; disk census of (beta*_3) Phi_0: {p_4}-disks 358,288, {p_1,p_4} 260,096, {p_1,p_2,p_4} 228,241, {p_3} 127,391, nested 4-disks 2,655, all of Lemma 6.1's types, with the 4-disks the ONLY parity violators; M_(Phi_0)(-t) has 488,336 plus and 488,336 minus coefficients and M_(Phi_0)(-1) = 0 exactly (as BBB-GAP.md Lemma 2 predicts). The Gamma construction (faces of D_5 minus diameter and beta, q in the innermost 4-disk outside all k <= 3 disks, gamma_1 by BFS never crossing beta with the paper's condition (3) as a wall, gamma_2 through the band crossing each 4-disk once) is implemented and reproduces the paper's own Gamma on its example exactly. Two-variable facts on the paper's example (weights t,t,t,t,u, canonical lifts): D(t,u) has t-range [-4,4], u-levels {-1,0}, and six collisions under u = t (so the one-variable specialization loses information, supporting Codex's refinement). Structural facts for the holder: for a simple Gamma, (beta*_3) Push(Gamma) is beta*_3 with a finger at each crossing with beta*_3 following Gamma to p_5 and back, so iota(Gamma) = 2 sum_k (number of alpha-crossings of Gamma after its k-th crossing of beta*_3) and M_Gamma's u-levels stay in {0, +-1}; since gamma_1 must parallel beta inside the innermost 4-disk, iota(Gamma) is much larger than iota(beta), while Lemma 2 forces M_(f(Phi) Gamma)(-1) = +-M_Gamma(-1) and Lemma 6.4 gives iota(f(Phi) Gamma) = iota(beta): together, parity must fail on the Gamma side whenever beta's only violators are 4-disks (a conditional argument, not a computed one). Precise obstacle: M_Gamma for such arcs needs a prefix-sum or transfer-matrix evaluation of the finger formula or rho(Push(Gamma)) from a compressed description of Gamma, plus a much smaller proper-product representative. Per Caleb's redirect the Burau_4 attack is closed here; the facts stay banked.

Codex 175633 (17:59 wall clock): first designs under the redirect. CX-C1-GATE (TRUSTED, exact): for two marked antiparallel full-twist sites replaced by I or e (e^2 = delta e), with corner brackets B00 = F(I,I), B10 = F(e,I), B01 = F(I,e), B11 = F(e,e) and kappa = (-A^3)^(-w0)/delta: a = kappa B11/delta^2, b = kappa (B01/delta - B11/delta^2), c = kappa (B10/delta - B11/delta^2), d = kappa (B00 - B10/delta - B01/delta + B11/delta^2); so b = c = 0 iff delta B10 = B11 = delta B01, and the whole line p + q = m is Jones-unit iff additionally B11 + (delta^2 B00 - B11) A^(8m) = (-A^3)^(w0) delta^3 (three denominator-free identities). "One turnback frees the other site" must prove the framed relation F(e, T^q) = A^(-6q) F(e, I) and its counterpart; two bands incident to the same components do not by themselves give it. If L is the inherited one-turnback link, a = V_L/delta and d = V_seed - a; only for split L = J_1 u J_2 is a = V_(J_1) V_(J_2). In the standard unlink-resolution mechanism V_(p,q) = 1 + (V_(0,0) - 1) A^(8(p+q)), so replacing the seed by a Jones-nontrivial determinant-one ribbon knot cannot give a unit line (REJECTED before computation), and an unknot seed in the homology-preserving construction gives only unknots (Khovanov detection). Cancelling the defect needs a changed turnback link, not relabelled components. Caution: delta = 0 at the determinant evaluation; cancel denominators first (work/global_design/c1_candidate_gate.md).
CX-H2 (OPEN, executable design; work/global_design/c4_two_site_candidate.md): the fixed EKT shell H(T,U) always has >= 2 components (sublinks T^N, U^N) and the archived cover torsion blocks any single rational replacement from giving determinant one, so the specimen changes two marked sites: with spherogram tangles T = IntegerTangle(3), U = IntegerTangle(-3), A0 = IdentityBraid(2) | T.rotate(1), B0 = U.rotate(1) | IdentityBraid(2), h = BraidTangle([-2,-3,-1,-2], 4), K(m,n) = braid closure of A0 h sigma_2 sigma_2^(2m) h sigma_2^(2n) B0; every K(m,n) is a knot (baseline permutation (12)(34), the fixed odd crossing makes a four-cycle, full twists preserve it); K(0,0): one component, 15 crossings, writhe -7; w = -7 + 2m + 2n; both sites coherent with winding 2 (sector exponents -12, -4). Corner table with the reduced bracket (<U> = 1), keeping the fixed sigma_2 and replacing the boxes by I or e_2: P = XY F00 + (1 - X) Y F10/delta + X (1 - Y) F01/delta + (1 - X)(1 - Y) F11/delta^2 with X = A^(8m), Y = A^(8n), V_K = (-A^3)^7 A^(-12(m+n)) P; unit locus: delta^2 P(A; A^(8m), A^(8n)) = delta^2 (-A^3)^(-7) A^(12(m+n)); first extract the bilinear determinant polynomial and reject if |det| = 1 has no integer solutions. Nothing beyond connectivity and writhe tested.

Codex 180646 (18:08 wall clock; read by Claude at sketch level): CX-MARKED (TRUSTED): for a diagram D with r disjoint marked identity two-string boxes, V_p = Col_p(D)/constants and d_i = colour difference of the two strands at site i, there is an injection ker(d_1, ..., d_r) -> V_p(output) for ARBITRARY two-string replacements at the sites (extend the common colour constantly through each replacement; the outside colouring is kept; nonconstant classes stay nonconstant), so dim V_p(output) >= dim V_p(D) - rank(d). For the baseline two-component H(3,-3) (cover H_1 = Z + Z/13, dim V_13 = 2; the baseline for CX-H2 is this link BEFORE the joining half twist, the first replacement being the whole odd braid sigma_2^(2m+1)), every determinant-one knot output requires det[d_i(f_j)] nonzero mod 13 for a reduced colouring basis f_1, f_2; a dependent pair of site functionals leaves a colouring surviving every replacement, making every determinant in the family divisible by 13. Placement rule: beside each crossing a small crossing-free identity box with difference d_c = o - u; if all d_c vanish the Fox relations force a constant colouring, so the crossing differences span V_p^*, and dim V_p independent sites can be chosen at distinct crossings; a mixed crossing with nonzero d_c, given one fixed odd braid, merges the components and full twists at the chosen sites keep one component. C1: no shell yet; Conway mutation in one four-port input preserves its whole TL_2 vector and all four B_ij; the opposite EKT operation preserves the Hopf pairing and the numerator-sum knot pairing, hence B00; neither changes a failed unit equation while keeping the three turnback identities; the missing object is a marked outside shell whose three turnback contractions share the required pairing while the seed contraction changes.

CX-H2 VERDICT (18:11 wall clock; Claude's certification agent, scratchpad cx-h2/, 136k tokens, 11 minutes): REJECTED at the determinant stage. Exact: V(K(m,n))(-1) = (-1)^(m+n) (13 + 26 m - 18 n - 88 m n) on the whole grid m, n in {-2..2} (fit verified on all 25 points; cross-checked against regina's Alexander polynomial at -1 and the HFK Euler characteristic), so det K(m,n) = |13 + 26m - 18n - 88mn|; writing -88 f = (-88m - 18)(-88n + 26) - 676, f = +1 needs (-88m - 18)(-88n + 26) = 588 = 2^2 3 7^2 and f = -1 needs 764 = 2^2 191, but the left factor is 70 mod 88 and no divisor of 588 or 764 is 70 mod 88: no integer solutions, no degenerate lines (brute force to |m|, |n| <= 5000 agrees). Hence no member has determinant 1 and none has V = 1. Everything else checked: all 25 K(m,n) are knots, K(0,0) has 15 crossings and writhe -7, w = -7 + 2m + 2n; the corner brackets F00 = -A^-13 + A^-9 - 2A^-5 + 2A^-1 - 3A^3 + 2A^7 - A^11 + A^15, F10 = A^-15 - A^-11 + 2A^-7 - 3A^-3 + 2A - 2A^5 + A^9 - A^13, F01 = -A^-7 + A^-3 - A + 2A^5 - 2A^9 + A^13 - A^17, F11 = -A^-13 + 2A^-9 - 3A^-5 + 4A^-1 - 4A^3 + 4A^7 - 2A^11 + 2A^15 (corner (1,1) a two-component link) reproduce the direct Jones polynomials EXACTLY at all 25 points through P = XY F00 + (1-X) Y F10/delta + X (1-Y) F01/delta + (1-X)(1-Y) F11/delta^2 and V = (-A^3)^7 A^(-12(m+n)) P (so the corner method and the normalization are validated); the direct bracket (planar contraction, exact, A-smoothing joining (a,b),(c,d) in the PD convention, fixed by the trefoil check) agrees exactly with regina's Jones at all 25 points. Example: V(K(0,0)) = -t^-9 + t^-8 - 2t^-7 + 3t^-6 - 2t^-5 + 2t^-4 - t^-3 + t^-2 (det 13), V(K(0,1)) = -t^-7 + t^-6 - t^-5 + t^-4 + t^-2 (det 5). The colouring gate does NOT reject the family: det = n(8 + 3m) mod 13, gcd of the grid determinants 1, so the site functionals are independent mod 13; the n = 0 slice det = 13 |2m + 1| matches the one-site torsion obstruction; the rejection is the finer divisor obstruction. Structural observation: at every HFK-checked point (K(0,0), K(0,1), K(1,1), K(-1,2)) the total knot Floer rank equals the determinant (13, 5, 67, 127; genera 2, 2, 4, 5), so the members are Floer-thin, and a Floer-thin knot with V = 1 has HFK rank det = 1 and is the unknot; likewise a Khovanov-thin knot with V = 1 has reduced Khovanov rank 1 and is the unknot (Kronheimer-Mrowka). CHECKLIST ADDITION: a counterexample must be both Khovanov-thick and knot-Floer-thick; in particular no quasi-alternating or thin family can contain one, and thin-looking templates (the H(3,-3) shell among them) are rejected in advance.

Codex 182306 (18:25 wall clock): CX-H2 closed with an independent arithmetic finish (f = 13 + 26m - 18n - 88mn, (-88m - 18)(-88n + 26) = 676 - 88 f; no signed divisor of 588 or 764 is 70 mod 88). Scope correction accepted: the four HFK-thin samples are pointwise data, not a proof that the family or its modifications are thin; the determinant rejection is what decides. CX-EKT-ANCHOR (constructed, then REJECTED without computing corners): with the literal EKT Hopf shell and omega operation, S_n = sigma_1^(-2n) R(25,-9) (fraction 25/(-9 + 50n)), T_n = omega(S_n) (fraction (3 + 400n)/(-2 + 150n)), L_n = H(T_n, -3): N(S_n) is one two-bridge knot J of determinant 25 for every n and V(L_n) = delta V_J (a nonunit Jones-flat turnback family); K(m,n) = closure((I_2 | rot(T_n)) h C_m h (rot(-3) | I_2)) with C_m = I_1 | rot(sigma_1^(2m)) | I_1 at the seam between the h blocks, a band fusion of L_n, one component for every parameter, w = -2m + 2n; K(0,0) (24 crossings, writhe 0) simplifies in spherogram to a crossing-free unknot (regina's simplifier stopped at 17 crossings; no independent simplification claimed). Raw corners: B00 = delta and delta B10 = B11 from the flat family, and E = delta B01 - B11 = 0 would give V_K(m,m) = 1 + (E/delta^3)(A^(8m) - 1) = 1. REJECTION by an all-parameter homology lemma (Claude's read: correct): if a link L with b_1(Sigma_2 L) = 1 bands to a knot K, the two double branched covers are Dehn fillings of one torus-boundary M (Montesinos); the finite K-filling forces b_1(M) = 1; with H_1(M) = Z + T the L-filling kills a torsion class, so Tor H_1(Sigma_2 L) is a quotient of T, while the K-filling has order |T| times a nonzero free-coordinate index; hence |Tor H_1(Sigma_2 L)| divides det K. Here H_1(Sigma_2 L_n) = Z + Z/|1 + 1800 n| (archived integral boundary calculation), so |1 + 1800n| divides det K(m,n) for every band framing m; for n = 0 the one-band recurrence V_K(m,0) = V_J + (1 - V_J) A^(8m) with V_J nonunit leaves only m = 0; the complete Jones-unit locus is (0,0), the unknot, so E is nonzero without computing it. General form: for S_n = p/(q + 2pn) (p odd, gcd(p,q) = 1), |Tor H_1(Sigma_2 H(omega(S_n), -3))| = |13p + 36q + 72pn|, equal to 1 for at most one n (two solutions would differ by 2/(72p)); so changing the nonunit rational seed cannot restore an infinite unit diagonal; a useful deformation must keep torsion-free homology along the entire proposed unit locus or change the outside construction (internal fixed-slope deformations of a nonrational seed not excluded). CX-LOCK-POS (Codex, TRUSTED on its side; Claude did not re-derive the sign step): for the matched-chain family Kan(2m, 2n, 2j, 2i) of Anokhina-Lanina-Morozov (EPJC 84 (2024) 990, eqs 7.20-7.21), the reduced Jones defect at x = q^2 is a positive monomial times z Q with [r] = (x^r - x^-r)/(x - x^-1), z = x^2 + x^-2 - x - x^-1, Q = [i][j](x^(i-j) + x^(j-i) + z[i][j]), and the bracketed factor is positive for every real x > 1 (same signs immediate; for i = a > 0, j = -b < 0 it is x^(a-b) + x^(b-a) + (x + x^-1 - 2)[a][b] in Codex's rewriting), so reduced Jones is 1 iff i = 0 or j = 0: no choice of m + n cancels a nondegenerate seed defect; zero-chain cases have reduced HOMFLY 1 but their unknot isotopies are unverified; unmatched chains and the larger KanGen family outside the calculation. The rotor check recovered only the archived APR question and its obstructions. Ask: no new computation; do not evaluate E; the Sakuma certification is the live test.

Codex 182850 (18:28 wall clock), CORRECTION accepted by Claude: "HFK rank = det implies Floer-thin" is FALSE. det K = |sum_(M,a) (-1)^(M+a) dim HFK_(M,a)(K)|, so rank = det means every supported delta-grading has the same PARITY, while thinness means a single delta-grading. Published counterexample: W = D_+(T_(2,3), 6), the six-twisted positive Whitehead double of the right-handed trefoil (Hedden-Ording math/0512348 Prop 2.1): HFK over F_2 has rank 25 with Delta_W = -6t + 13 - 6t^-1, det 25, but delta-gradings 0 (rank 17) and 2 (rank 8): thick; it is the thick A-knot of Kotelskiy-Watson-Zibrowius 2105.06308 Def 1.3, Ex 7.15. So the four CX-H2 samples are verified HFK A-knots (rank = det), not verified thin. CHECKLIST, corrected: a counterexample must FAIL both A-knot conditions, HFK rank different from det (else rank 1 and Ozsvath-Szabo unknot detection) and reduced-Khovanov rank over Q different from det (else rank 1 and Kronheimer-Mrowka); thick is necessary but weaker. DEAD_ENDS: "rank = det implies thin" REFUTED by the Whitehead double. No recomputation needed.

Codex 183430 (18:33 wall clock), CX-LOCK-POS completed and CX-LOCK-GEN rejected (recorded; Codex's diagram traces, two internal readers; Claude did not re-trace the moves): in the matched-chain family Kan(2m, 2n, 2j, 2i) of Anokhina-Lanina-Morozov (arXiv:2407.08724, Figure 25), the zero-chain cases are actual unknots by moves: for i = 0 the small upper return arc caps the 2n chain (remove by RI), the cup-transfer isotopy sigma_1^r C = sigma_3^r C with the nested cup system C = (14)(23) moves one j chain around the cups to cancel its inverse by RII, the 2m chain is then capped and removed by RI, leaving a planar long arc; for j = 0 the same upside down. With the positivity result: in Kan(2m,2n,2j,2i), V = 1 iff i j = 0 iff the knot is U. The even-parameter KanGen enlargement with vertical-edge counts k, l >= 0, h = k + l: at HOMFLY A = q^2, H/D = M^h + q^(-4S) L^h Z Q with S = m + n, M = q(q^4 + 1), L = -q^5 (q^2 - 1), Z = (q^3 - q^-3)(q - q^-1), Q = Q_(i,j)(q^2); at q = 1 this is 2^h, so for h > 0 these are (h + 1)-component links, not knots; h = 0 is the matched-chain family. Negative vertical counts have no geometric definition. Both published families are closed as counterexample sources; no reason to enlarge any parameter scan.

Codex 184230 (18:42 wall clock), two construction mechanisms tried and closed (recorded; Codex's classifications, not re-derived here). CX-NET (REJECTED for the specified construction): signed-Potts star-triangle replacement of a piece of an unknot by a network with the same full boundary partition function; archived five-channel condition abc = Q(Q + a + b + c) with Q = 2 - y - y^-1, y = -A^4, triangle weights Q/a, Q/b, Q/c, scalar factor Q + a + b + c; for integer parallel twist bundles a_n = y^n - 1 and their series duals Q/a_n, the full identity holds exactly when the three effective weights include a_1 and a_-1 (four cases: degree comparison, cyclotomic multiplicities, exponent multisets); the surviving solutions are the tangle identities sigma_1 sigma_2^n sigma_1^-1 = sigma_2^-1 sigma_1^n sigma_2 (the braid relation and its conjugated powers) and their rotations, so these replacements never change a knot type (Kauffman's signed Tutte polynomial for the correspondence; work/global_design/potts_twist_gate.md). Not classified: rational-tangle arms, singular reductions, larger networks, equality after one closure only. CX-ANTISYM (braided version REJECTED): a knot modification whose entire HOMFLY defect lies in the three-strand antisymmetric channel would need [beta] - u I in that Hecke ideal, making beta projectively scalar in the generic TL_3 image, hence central in B_3, beta = Delta^(2k) with TL eigenvalues A^(+-6k), scalar only for k = 0; no nontrivial three-strand braid realizes it (single-projector obstruction already in work/research45/quantum_identities.txt); nonbraid tangles or closure-specific cancellation not excluded, none supplied.

Codex 190340 (19:04 wall clock): CX-SAK5-A, independent check of Claude's first n = 5 output (prefix n5_inv1_I1_2_2_m10_5 in scratchpad cx-sak/out): the 181- and 209-crossing projections have identical saved Jones dictionaries, 29 terms, extremes -t^(-11/2) ... + t^(45/2), span 28 against span 1 for U_2; Regina 7.4 simplifies the first diagram to 97 crossings and independently computes exactly that polynomial (no mirror or framing adjustment); in both PDs the axis has no self-crossings and the other component simplifies by actual isotopy (from 161 and 189 crossings) to a crossing-free unknot; source-knot checks V(1) = 1, V'(1) = V''(1) = 0, V(-1) = -799; in F_16, V_S = d and V_kappa = 1, so FROB5 passes (the sieve never predicted global equality). So the supplied diagram's polynomial test is decisively NEGATIVE for inversion A; final identification of the diagram with the specified marked Sakuma input stays with Claude's certification; no verdict inferred for inversion B. CX-PANTS (conditional transfer, TRUSTED with internal reads, source OPEN/PARKED): let P be an embedded pair of pants with zero Seifert form and three unknotted boundary components; cut it to a disk along two disjoint proper arcs and put m, n full twists in the two cut boxes; the surface supplies the four closures V00 = 1, S1 = S2 = delta (single-cut surfaces are zero-framed unknotted annuli), S12 = V_boundary(P); with z = A^8 and consistent handedness V_K(m,n) = 1 + (1 - z^m)(1 - z^n)(V_boundary(P)/delta^2 - 1), so for mn nonzero K(m,n) is Jones-unit exactly when the boundary 3-component link is Jones-unlink; nontriviality certificate: Gamma = K00 u (two marked crossing circles) is Brunnian (each proper pair an unlink), and if Gamma is nontrivial then every K(m,n) with mn nonzero is nontrivial, since otherwise filling the circles and +1 surgery on the alleged unknot gives S^3 by finite nonmeridional surgery on a nontrivial homologically trivial Brunnian link, against Mangum-Stanford Thm 2 (AGT 2001); for the canonical pants of a Brunnian theta graph, Huh Thm 8 / Cor 9 identify the boundary equality with the planar normalized Yamada value, so a marked theta input with that value and nontrivial Gamma gives the counterexample K(1,1). Missing inputs: no nontrivial theta with the required Yamada value found (the known equal-Yamada example has a nonunit factor); nonplanarity alone does not certify Gamma nontrivial. Parked; no theta sweep. (Claude's note: EKT's Jones-unlink links have knotted components, so they do not meet the unknotted-boundary hypothesis directly.)

Codex 191228 (19:16 wall clock), CX-PANTS strengthened (TRUSTED; read and confirmed by Claude): for a NONPLANAR Brunnian theta curve Theta with canonical pants P (zero Seifert form, three unknotted boundary components), the two-band twisted knots K(m,n) with mn nonzero are nontrivial unconditionally, every K(m,n) has determinant one, and K(1,1) has unknotting number exactly one; so the only remaining input is a nonplanar Brunnian theta with the planar normalized Yamada value, and then K(1,1) is a Jones-unit nontrivial knot with u = 1. Proof of nontriviality (Claude's read): Gamma = K00 u C_a u C_b (unknot plus the two crossing circles) is Brunnian by the annulus cuts; if Gamma were an unlink then in the double cover branched over K00 (S^3) the four lifts of C_a, C_b form an unlink (splitting balls disjoint from K00), the crossing disks lift to disjoint annuli A_a, A_b whose cores kappa_a, kappa_b are isotopic to boundary components, hence an unlink with zero annular framings; restoring each cut strip is annular-longitude surgery on its lifted core (local marking: no extra +-1, since after the other planar smoothing the middle crossing disk misses the branch set and its lifted boundary circles bound disks in the new filling), so Sigma_2(L) = S^3_(0,0)(U_2) = #^2 (S^1 x S^2); Plotnick's unlink-cover classification (as stated in Gorchakov 2408.12557 Thm 2.15) gives L = U_3 and Kauffman-Simon-Wolcott-Zhao section 3 then gives Theta planar, a contradiction; so Gamma is nontrivial and Mangum-Stanford Thm 2 makes every K(m,n) with mn nonzero nontrivial. Determinant: the zero Seifert matrix gives H_1(Sigma_2(L)) = Z^2; the two integral smoothing surgeries on kappa_a u kappa_b have symmetric linking matrix B with coker B = Z^2, so B = 0 (zero framings, zero linking); the cover of K(m,n) is the filling with slopes mu +- 2m lambda, mu +- 2n lambda (coefficients +-1/(2m), +-1/(2n); zero parameter means meridional), numerator one and zero linking give H_1 = 0, so det K(m,n) = 1 for all parameters (upstairs coefficients differ from the downstairs +-1/m). u(K11) = 1: one crossing change in the first box cancels the pair, giving K01 = U. Scope: the four-closure identity V_K(m,n) - 1 = (1 - A^(8m))(1 - A^(8n))(V_L - delta^2)/delta^2 and Huh Thm 8 / Cor 9 (normalized Jaeger specialization = normalized Yamada) make K11 Jones-unit iff Theta has the planar value; this is the Lemma-Q / u = 1 slice. Claude's unification note, with Codex's scope corrections (193246): K11 Jones-unit iff its first crossing-circle link (K01 = U) u C_a is Jones-trivial (Theorem 4), so CX-PANTS builds Jones-trivial crossing-circle (Sakuma) links from Yamada-trivial Brunnian theta curves; but the quotient theta of a strong inversion has only ONE guaranteed unknot constituent (Brunnianness is an extra condition), the new theta of the construction has not been identified with the quotient theta of an arbitrary inversion, and Huh's formula involves the other constituents, so the six Sakuma rejections are NOT automatically Yamada verdicts. Also: in the determinant argument the lifted core pair is algebraically split and zero-framed, not necessarily an unlink (unlink only under the hypothetical Gamma-unlink assumption); algebraic splitting is all the numerator-one homology argument needs. No polynomial-flat nonplanar theta known; no theta sweep.

CX-SAK5 VERDICTS (19:18 wall clock; Claude's certification agent, scratchpad cx-sak/, copied to ~/jones-lanes/cx-tools/cx-sak/; 461k tokens, 76 minutes): ALL SIX pilot candidates REJECTED: the zero-framed Sakuma links of kappa_n = [2,-4n,2,2n] for n = 5, 10, 15 and both inversions I_1(2,2;-2n,n), I_1(-2n,4n;-1,-1) have Jones polynomials different from the two-component unlink's. Codex 192805 (19:31 wall clock), CX-BAND3 (marked candidate, REJECTED exactly; crossed Claude's 19:18 letter): the pants package extended to a disk with r bands, assuming every proper restored subsurface is ambiently standard, gives the r-cube identity V_K(n) - 1 = prod_i (1 - A^(8 n_i)) (V_boundary(P)/delta^r - 1) (all proper sectors supplied by topology; only the joint sector can block flatness); Gamma is Brunnian, the disjoint-lifted-annuli argument extends to r (Gamma unlink => boundary unlink), so a nontrivial boundary certifies the nonzero-parameter knots nontrivial by Mangum-Stanford; zero Seifert form gives determinant one, and for r >= 3 every Seifert entry is visible on a proper one- or two-band surface, so the marked proper-surface hypothesis forces the zero form. r = 3 with the pure braid beta = [sigma_1^2, sigma_2^2] in the three ribbons (deleting any band deletes a strand and kills beta, so all proper marked surfaces are standard); boundary marking: cable each positive sigma_i to (2i, 2i-1, 2i+1, 2i) and inverses to the inverse word, close by top2-top3, top4-top5, bottom2-bottom3, bottom4-bottom5, top1-bottom1, top6-bottom6 (reduced Markov trace of cable(beta) e_2 e_4), exterior surface the disk at infinity; 32 crossings, writhe 0. Codex 200256 (20:09 wall clock), CHALLENGE accepted: CX-DESIGN-1-LOBES is WITHDRAWN. Scharlemann's band-sum theorem needs a SPLIT starting link (Taylor, PJM 241 (2009) p. 129 states the hypothesis); for a nonsplit link the band fusion to the unknot can have knotted components: the zero-framed annulus around a trefoil has boundary two trefoils with lk 0, and the band along a spanning arc gives the boundary of a disk, the actual unknot. So the correct u = 1 target has NO unknotted-component restriction: a two-component link L with V(L) = V(U_2) exactly (lk 0) that is the turnback of an unknot at a site (band-sums to U); "Jones detects the unlink among two-unknot-component links implies Lemma Q" still holds through the Sakuma equivalence (whose components are unknotted by construction), not through the lobes. Exact answers: (ii) in the archived EKT orbit L_j = H(Omega^j S, Omega^-j(infinity + 2)) with Omega^j = [[a, 8b],[b, a]], a^2 - 8b^2 = 1, for any two-tangle S with S^N = U (not necessarily rational) normalized to primitive pair (1, d): the slot pairs are (a + 8bd, b + ad) and (a, -b), the components have determinants |a + 8bd| and a, so for j nonzero both components are knotted (|a + 8bd| >= 5|b| > 1 for d nonzero, = a >= 3 for d = 0); H_1(Sigma_2 L_j) = Z + Z/|1 + 12b(b + ad)| and D = 1 + 12b(b + ad) = 1 mod 12 forces D = 1 for torsion-freeness, i.e. b = 0 or b = -ad, and coprimality plus Pell force a = 1, b = 0: torsion-free iff j = 0 iff the unlink; no residual congruence classes. Framing: even j gives exactly the unlink Jones polynomial, odd j does so for d odd and gives t^(+-6) times it for d even (EKT Thms 3.2', 4.1, 4.3). The (11,-4) proposal fails: that cap-twist template's link polynomial is delta V_J with det J = |p|, so Jones-unlink needs |p| = 1, not 11 or 25 mod 36. (iii) CX-HOPF-UU (TRUSTED): with br(T) = (f, g), br(U) = (F, G), numerator brackets n_T = delta f + g, n_U = delta F + G, EKT's Prop 2.1' factors as <H(T,U)> = delta n_T n_U + (h - delta^3) f F with h - delta^3 = -(A^6 - A^-6)(A^8 - A^-8) nonzero; if both numerator closures are actual unknots and V_H = V(U_2) exactly then lk = 0, all eight shell crossings are mixed so w_H = w_T + w_U, the raw target bracket is delta u_T u_U, and the identity forces f F = 0: one input is a framing-unit multiple of infinity with an unknot numerator closure; if rational it is infinity and the shell splits (U_2); a nonsplit solution would already contain a nonrational flat input with an actual U closure, a Lemma Q counterexample. So two nonflat unknot-numerator inputs cannot cancel in the Hopf shell; the remaining case is exactly the existing crux. Also: wrapping 4 is not automatically the pants package (the meridional cut gives e_0, e_2, e_4 sectors but not the marked balls, embedded pants, framings and partial closures); and the K111 class audit is incomplete (semiadequate, Montesinos, three-braid, Whitehead-double membership and a crossing number above 24 not certified).

Codex 201900 (20:20 wall clock): two corrections to Claude's CX-THROUGH note, accepted. (1) A flat integral two-tangle has the e endpoint pairing: if [Y] = y e with r closed components, the two closures have component counts (r + 1, r + 2) and |y(1)| = 2^r (magnitudes of y delta and y delta^2 at A = 1 against 2^(components)); the trace closure of an id-matching tangle has MORE components than its other closure, so it cannot be flat. Hence the target is: e-matching, NO closed components (r = 0), unit y, nontrivial embedding (not isotopic to e rel boundary, i.e. nonrational), and then the trace closure is a knot (counterexample or unknot). A locally knotted e is already a flat nonrational tangle with y the local knot's Jones polynomial up to framing, so flatness alone is not enough. EKT's flat complements have r = 1 (the untouched other component), giving the (2,3) counts; opening that component by a seam fusion cuts the content itself: the right object then has six ends and a rank-five TL_3 vector whose cap projection is the old flat identity, the other coordinates unknown; so Claude's "EKT content acts as scalars under planar operations" holds only for EXTERNAL composition, not for fusions through the content. CX-THROUGH REFUTED as stated; the no-closed-component formulation retained. (2) CX-GRAPH (TRUSTED): do not enumerate algebraic shells for the actual-unknot-anchored flat target: the double cover of an algebraic (arborescent) tangle X is a graph manifold (Sikora section 3), an actual U filling makes it E(kappa) with kappa a graph knot, graph knots are generated from U by cabling and connected sums (Budney-Cohen section 10), and a_2(C_(p,q)(J)) = p^2 a_2(J) + (p^2 - 1)(q^2 - 1)/24 with additivity gives a_2(kappa) > 0 for every nontrivial graph knot, while U4 gives a_2(kappa) = 0 on the anchored-flat locus; so kappa = U and X is rational; anchored-flat designs need a hyperbolic JSJ piece upstairs (not an exclusion of arborescent Jones-unit knots without the actual U closure). (3) CX-EKT-TENSOR (TRUSTED): for a compensated two-slot orbit in reduced closed-bracket conventions, with C the four marked shell values and F = (delta^2 - 2)(delta^2 - 4), universal opposite-Omega preservation holds exactly when C01 = C10 = r and C00 = delta (delta^2 - 3)^2 r - F C11, i.e. C = r G + E diag(-F, 1) with E = C11 - delta r, G = [[delta,1],[1,delta]]; an exact finite test needing no rational pairs; the 00 corner cannot be a knot (C00(1) = -2 r(1)); corner 11 can. Target tensor: C = H/delta = [[delta^6 - 7delta^4 + 15delta^2 - 8, delta],[delta, 1]] (all four Hopf corners divided by delta; component pattern (3,2,2,1); integral; satisfies both equations); a marked band realizing this whole tensor with actual corner 11 = U would give raw-unit knot members under even opposite-Omega iterates, decisive if any is nontrivial; no band realizing C has been constructed; the rejected anchor's corners fail their identities. Ask: from the running survey, report any marked shell's four rational-slot corners passing the two tensor equations (especially H/delta) with the unknot-seed certificate, or the nonzero tensor defects with markings; rational-slot C corners are not the full-twist-site B corners. (Claude's survey agent re-briefed to compute both, with the unfused shell as calibration.)

Codex 203900 (20:40 wall clock), CX-EKT-FUSE1 (TRUSTED; surgery explanation of the survey's common defect; H/delta target WITHDRAWN/REFUTED): four marked rational-slot closures with a determinant-one 11 corner lift to two simultaneous integral surgeries in an integral homology sphere, the two crossingless filling slopes at distance one, with linking matrix [[a, l],[l, b]]: the single-corner determinants are |a|, |b| and the double-corner determinant |ab - l^2| (the 11 corner need not be an actual unknot). For H/delta the determinants would be (8, 0, 0, 1), forcing a = b = 0 and l^2 = 8: impossible, so that tensor is unrealizable despite integrality and component counts. More generally, for ANY one fixed band outside both input balls in the original Hopf shell with fused 11 corner a determinant-one knot: the original 01 and 10 corners are U_3 (EKT section 2), each fused single corner is a two-component link of linking number 0 (the band joins two unlink components, their sum has zero linking with the third), its Conway polynomial is z^3 P(z^2), so 8 divides its determinant, hence 8 | a, b; universal opposite-Omega invariance needs C00 = -8 C11 at delta = 0, i.e. double-corner determinant 8, but |ab - l^2| = 8 would need l^2 = 8 mod 16: impossible. This excludes every external single band (knotted or not) in that position, not just the canonical ones surveyed. The survey's seam-band tensor at zeta_8 is [[4,0],[0,1]]: zero framings, |l| = 2, double determinant 4 against the demanded -8, defect 12; the crossing fusions give the same specialized tensor up to their unit and the same normalized defect 12 (all six saved tensors checked exactly in Z[zeta_8] from rational_slots.json; the unfused control passes). This explains nonvanishing for every eligible band, not literal equality of the full defect polynomials. Constraint for a genuinely CHANGED shell with unit 11 corner, two-component off-diagonal corners and the invariant tensor: with common single determinant d (even), |a| = |b| = d and |ab - l^2| = 8; the same-sign case is impossible (squares mod 4 after dividing by 4) and the opposite-sign case forces d = 2, |l| = 2, a = -b = +-2: the single corners must be determinant-2 two-component links (Hopf-type partial closures) with opposite surgery signs; unlink corners and the linking-zero corners of the old one-band fusion cannot work. Also: the unfused table's C/delta was a formal extra division; the actual reduced tensor H is realizable with pattern (4,3,3,2). Asks: close the H/delta and single-band adapter tasks (done); for the next changed-shell design apply the determinant-2/opposite-sign partial-filling requirement before any bracket computation; a proposed diagram must change the partial closures, not wind the old band farther. Claude's follow-up design (CX-CLASP, below): the invariant tensors form the two-parameter family C = [[delta r - F E, r],[r, delta r + E]]; with the 11 corner a unit u and the single corners a common r, the double corner is forced: C00 = delta (delta^2 - 3)^2 r - F u. With Hopf-link single corners (r the Hopf bracket) and an unknot 11 corner this is one explicit Laurent polynomial, the bracket any realizing shell's three-component double corner must have (up to units); identifying that link among small three-component links of determinant 8 is a finite decisive computation, launched. Candidate geometry: an unknot with two clasped fingers (each finger pushed through the main loop and clasped) has single turnbacks equal to Hopf links of chosen opposite signs and 11 corner an unknot, but with far-apart fingers the lifted slot arcs are unlinked (l = 0) and the double corner is the three-chain of determinant 4; the fingers must be entangled so that l = +-2 (double corner determinant 8).

Codex 205400 (20:55 wall clock), on CX-CLASP, with CX-OMEGA-ORBIT and CX-SHAH-PARTIAL. (1) Relative framing rule (necessary, termwise): for four raw brackets from one fixed two-slot diagram, changing one basis smoothing shifts the common A-degree class by 2 mod 4 (the number of state circles changes by one), changing both shifts it back; so if C11 = u is a monomial, every exponent of C01/u and C10/u is 2 mod 4 and every exponent of C00/u is 0 mod 4. (2) CX-OMEGA-ORBIT (TRUSTED): for nonzero fixed input bracket vectors v, w and s_n = v^T (Omega^T)^n C Omega^-n w, three equal raw values at distinct n force Omega^T C Omega^-1 = C (unless the orbit is constant a prescribed raw value occurs at most twice; same for even iterates; four consecutive values u z^n with z = +-A^k force z = 1 and invariance; for normalized Jones only after fixing the linear writhe increment). Proof: det Omega = -A^-6, its trace has six Laurent monomials, a rational eigenvalue would be a Laurent unit and two such cannot give that trace, so each nonzero vector has two nonzero eigencoordinates; the eigenvalue ratio r = -A^20(1 - 2A^-4 + ...) is no power monomial or torsion; the scalar orbit is d + p r^n + q r^-n; three equal values make a quadratic have three roots, so p = q = 0 and the off-diagonal entries of C in the eigenbasis vanish; four geometric monomial values by a four-base Vandermonde. So a fixed external EKT band cannot evade CX-EKT-FUSE1 by preservation on an entire orbit; a finite check is decisive; isolated coincidences, n-dependent bands and larger relative tangles remain outside. CX-CLASP identification (21:09 wall clock; Claude's agent, scratchpad cx-clasp/ copied to ~/jones-lanes/cx-tools/cx-clasp/; exact; 239k tokens): with u = 1 and admissible single corners r = eps A^m (-A^4 - A^-4), m = 2 mod 4, the forced double corner C00*(m, eps) = eps A^m T1 - F with T1 = delta (delta^2 - 3)^2 (-A^4 - A^-4) = A^14 - A^10 + 2A^6 + 2A^-6 - A^-10 + A^-14 and F = A^8 - 2A^4 + 2 - 2A^-4 + A^-8; all twelve candidates (m in {-10,...,10}, both signs) have exponents 0 mod 4 and C00*(zeta_8) = -8 exactly (determinant 8); the bare m = 0 corner has mixed exponent classes and is not a bracket of anything. Hoste-Thistlethwaite scan of all 109 prime three-component links through 10 crossings (brackets cross-checked by the naive state sum, determinants against Seifert matrices): determinant 8 occurs only for L10n84 (lk 2, 3) and L10n87 (lk 2, -3), and neither nor any mirror matches any candidate, nor the extended family |m| <= 30. Composites and hand candidates (three-chain det 4, (2,2,2)-pretzel = L6a5 det 12, Borromean det 16, L6n1 = T(3,3) det 4, T(2,4) # T(2,4) det 16, L2a1 # L7n1 det 8, split links det 0, all pure three-braid finger configurations): no match EXCEPT T(2,+4) # Hopf for (m, eps) = (-6, +1), with unit exactly 1 (C00*(-6,+1) = t^-1 + 2t - t^2 + 2t^3 - t^4 + t^5 = V(T(2,4) # Hopf), lk pattern (2, -1, 0); explicit 6-crossing PD verified), and its mirror T(2,-4) # Hopf for (+6, +1). The two realizable framings (m = -+6) are exactly those produced by the 2-crossing Hopf diagrams: turning back a bigon site of the closure of sigma_1^(+-2) gives an unknot diagram with u = A^(-+6). By-product: V'(1) = 6 lk_total forces lk_total odd for every candidate, so the picture "two fingers through one loop, fingers linked twice" (lk pattern 1, 1, 2) is excluded at every crossing number. Conclusion: a shell with a Hopf single corner and a double corner of at most 10 crossings must have C00 = T(2, -+4) # Hopf (one finger's tip links the main loop twice, the other's links that tip once and the main loop not at all).
Claude's analysis of the framing and sign constraint (recorded for Codex's check): in one diagram the single corner's writhe is w_01 = w_11 + 4 lk_01 (mixed crossings flip under the lobe reversal, their sign sum being -2 lk), so r/u = (-A^3)^(4 lk) V_L: for a positive Hopf corner (lk +1) r/u = A^12 (-A^-2 - A^-10) = A^6 (-A^4 - A^-4), m = +6, and for a negative one m = -6, matching the identification. E1 (C01 = C10 exactly) then forces the two single corners to have EQUAL raw brackets, which two genuine Hopf links achieve only with the same sign (the m = +6 and m = -6 polynomials are mirror images, not equal); but Codex's determinant analysis needs opposite surgery signs (same sign gives |4 - l^2| = 8, impossible). Hence NO shell with two genuine Hopf single corners can satisfy the invariant tensor. The way out, consistent with both: one genuine Hopf corner and one FAKE Hopf corner, a determinant-2 two-component link with the same raw bracket as the Hopf corner (V_fake = t^-6 V(Hopf^+) with lk = -1 against a positive Hopf, or the mirror pairing) and the opposite surgery sign; Shah's fake Hopf link P(-2,3,4) (det 2, shifted Hopf Jones value) is the natural candidate and is being checked exactly. Geometric candidate for the four closures with genuine Hopf corners (now known to fail E1, kept as the template to modify): an unknot X with two fingers, finger 1's tip Y wrapping X's strand twice (a double clasp, retractable) and finger 2's tip Z clasping Y once with the opposite sign; cutting finger 2 gives Z u (X with finger 1) = Hopf, cutting finger 1 gives Y u (X with finger 2) = Hopf after retracting the ear (Y becomes a (1,1) torus curve), cutting both gives T(2,4) u (meridian of Y) = T(2,4) # Hopf, and the uncut diagram is an unknot (retract finger 2 along finger 1, then finger 1).

Codex 211130 (21:13 wall clock), CX-BOX-SQUARE (TRUSTED; read and confirmed by Claude): local signed-square theorem. Let C be the four reduced raw brackets from one planar diagram with two disjoint marked smoothing boxes, outside framing fixed, with |C11(zeta_8)| = 1 and u = C11(zeta_8). Then C01/u = i a, C10/u = i b, C00/u = d and det(C)/u^2 = d + ab = m^2 with a, b, d, m integers: a POSITIVE square. Proof: complete the boxes by auxiliary crossings and take the common black Tait graph; at delta = 0 the contributing states are its spanning trees; with b_1, b_2 the memberships of the marked edges, C_b = q i^(-b_1 - b_2) tau_b where q is an eighth-root unit and T(x, y) = tau_00 + tau_10 x + tau_01 y + tau_11 x y is the integer signed spanning-tree polynomial (every tree has v - 1 edges and the black-IN/OUT ratio at an outside crossing is i times the edge sign); the reduced Laplacian is M + x p p^T + y s s^T and the rank-two determinant identity gives tau_10 tau_01 - tau_00 tau_11 = (p^T adj(M) s)^2 = m^2 (Claude: standard, tau_11 = [(p^T adj M p)(s^T adj M s) - (p^T adj M s)^2]/det M); hence det(C_b) = q^2 m^2, label reversals contribute (-1)^k to both det and u^2 and cancel; singular M, marked loops and bridges covered by the polynomial identity. Universal opposite-Omega invariance at zeta_8 gives d = -8 (F(zeta_8) = 8) and a = b, so a^2 - m^2 = 8, i.e. (a - m)(a + m) = 8, forcing |a| = 3, |m| = 1: the single corners must be KNOTS of determinant 3; two-component single corners have even determinant and a^2 - 8 = 8 or 12 mod 16 is never a square. CONSEQUENCES: CX-CLASP and CX-CLASP-2 REFUTED at every crossing number, genuine or fake Hopf corners alike (Claude's E1-versus-sign exclusion confirmed by this stronger argument; the writhe-to-surgery-sign inference not banked separately); the forced-corner identification is compatible (the polynomial belongs to a real link, but the four marked closures cannot coexist); CX-EKT-FUSE1's unsigned changed-shell guidance superseded. Surviving necessary pattern: component pattern (2,1,1,1) (coherent slots: single turnbacks are knots, the double turnback a two-component link), determinant pattern (8,3,3,1), C00 = -2r at A = 1; realized by ordinary rational gluing C = G Omega with C11 = -A^-15, C01 = C10 = -A^-1 - A^-9 + A^-13, C00 = A^9 - A^5 + 2A - A^-3 + 2A^-7 - A^-11 (tensor identity and Omega^T C = C Omega checked exactly), a CALIBRATION only: its Jones-unit rational closures are unknots; a new design needs actual marked nonrational geometry with the rational tensor and a reason its unit closure is outside detected classes. Shah: one marked replacement, no second compatible band, and now excluded with any genuine Hopf partner. Fake-Hopf parts-list computation STOPPED at Codex's request.
WHERE THE COUNTEREXAMPLE ROUTES STAND (21:13, Claude's synthesis for Caleb): every constructive route specified today has been reduced, by exact arguments, either to Lemma Q's crux (a flat nonrational tangle with no closed component and an unknot trace closure: Sakuma links, theta curves, pants, single-site flatness; all Jones-unlink-link mechanisms with knotted components excluded, algebraic tangles excluded) or to the faithfulness rungs (two-slot Omega-invariant shells with a unit seed need a nonrational shell with the rational tensor, i.e. a tangle bracket-equivalent to a rational one; constant-sum templates need a non-categorified double identity; the genus-two kernel needs a Torelli coincidence of spin lines with at least five lines spanning; Burau_4's published proof has a gap). The most direct constructive door left is Bigelow's criterion for Burau_4: a pair of arcs in the four-punctured disk with vanishing Burau pairing gives a kernel element, hence (Ito) an explicit nontrivial knot with V = 1; the day's results constrain such a pair (the braid relating them is in the t = -1 kernel and deep in the (t + 1)-adic filtration, the BBB-RETURN and BBB-PERRON families are detected and so excluded as shapes, and the multiset/localization structure of the Moody polynomial is what any vanishing pairing must defeat). No arc-pair search has been run today.

## X. RESTART (15 November 2026): theory-building mandate; lanes 1 to 3 (Claude, letter 50)

Caleb's mandate (verbatim in SCAFFOLD): build theory that explains the problem and delivers reductions; T is secondary. Status of every statement below: DERIVED by Claude on 15 November unless marked otherwise; conventions of the multicurve calculus are being pinned against the papers (workflow wf_c782585a-b7d); novelty unchecked.

X.1 The defect identity. For a knot K and a field F, BN~(K) = F[H]{0,s} + Tors, Tors a sum of bars F[H]/(H^k_i) generated in bidegree (h_i,q_i), deg H = (0,-2). The long exact sequence of the cone of H gives Kh~ generators at (h_i,q_i) and (h_i - 1, q_i - 2 k_i) for each bar, so

    V~_K(q) = q^s + (1 - q^-2) chi_q(Tors BN~(K)),   chi_1(Tors) = -s/2.

Checked by hand on 3_1 (one bar, k = 1, h odd), 4_1 (two bars of opposite sign), T(3,4) (bars of length 1 and 2, both odd: -3 = -s/2). T is the statement that a nonzero Tors never has chi_q(Tors) = (1 - q^s)/(1 - q^-2). The same algebra holds for HFK^- over F[U], where the analogue is false (Delta = 1 knots): the module structure alone proves nothing.

X.2 Unit bars. Torsion order is bounded by the proper rational unknotting number (Alishahi; Iltgen-Lewark-Marino; exact statements pending). On the Lemma Q family K_n = X cup sigma^n, n even, all bars have length one. For n odd the replacement is not proper (connectivity changes) and long bars occur: K_1 = T(3,5) for the trefoil has an H^2 bar.

X.3 The multicurve dictionary (to be pinned). Coordinates of Kotelskiy-Watson-Zibrowius arXiv:2104.13592: unknot slope infinity, Seifert longitude slope 0, K_n at slope 1/n. In the planar cover R^2 - Z^2 the arc a(infinity) is the image of a whole non-special lattice line, and any curve of another slope crosses it; since the unknot closure has rank one, exactly one component of the multicurve of X = T_(kappa,h) meets a(infinity), once (the arc component a(k), k an integer), and all other components are compact curves confined to the strip around the SPECIAL line of slope infinity. This compact part Gamma_c is Watson's kappa in curve form. Tors BN~(K_n) = HF(Gamma_c, a(1/n)) (plus a two-bridge term if k is nonzero). For n even the endpoints of a(1/n) lie off the special line, so Gamma_c cannot wind around them and bars have length one; for n odd one endpoint lies on it. This reproduces X.2, which is the reason to trust the picture. The graded count depends only on the class [Gamma_c] = C(q) v in a rank-one submodule of the rank-two bracket module; x = C + (correction from k); Lemma Q iff C + correction is nonzero for nontrivial inversions; preferred framing gives delta | x (a balance law between the two delta-classes of components). Each component pairs delta-thinly with an arc, so all cancellation is between components whose delta gradings differ by 2 mod 4. The formal countermodel of 3 November (an Euler-zero complex with identity transitions) must now be a union of linear curves in one strip with class zero: the missing theorem is a geography theorem for linear components (arXiv:2202.01460).

X.4 Hermitian calculus. [X] = x id + y e; Y* = reflection of Y in the Conway sphere; <X cup Y*> = [X]^T G conj[Y], G = [[delta^2, delta],[delta, delta^2]]; the braid action on the ends preserves G. Gram identity: <DX><DY> - <X cup Y*><Y cup X*> = delta^2 (delta^2 - 1) W conj(W), W = x_X y_Y - y_X x_Y, DX = X cup X*. If the id-closure of X has unit bracket: <DX> = 1 + (delta^2 - 1) y conj(y); the double has unlink bracket iff y is a monomial iff X is Jones-rational. For X = T_(kappa,h): DX is a two-component symmetric union with unknotted components, Sigma_2(DX) = S^3_0(kappa # -kappa). Lemma Q iff unlink detection on these doubles.

X.5 Period picture. det G = delta^2 (delta^2 - 1): definite for |delta| > 1, signature (1,1) for |delta| < 1 (t on the arc through -1 between the primitive cube roots). At t = -e^{2 pi i/n}, n >= 7, |delta| = 2 sin(pi/n) and the image of B_3 in PU(1,1) is the triangle group (2,3,n); rational tangles are the order-n vertices; for rational Q, Q': |<Q cup Q'*>| = delta^2 cosh(d/2), unknot closures = adjacent vertices (cosh(d/2) = 1/|delta|, the leg of the (2,3,n) triangle). Numerical confirmation to six digits: |V(T(2,k))| = |delta| cosh(d_k/2) with cosh d_k = cosh^2(2a) - sinh^2(2a) cos(2 pi k/n), cosh a = 1/(2 sin(pi/n)), for k = 1,2,3,5 and n = 7,8,9,12,30. Corollary: one root of unity detects the unknot among two-bridge knots. A general tangle gives a point P_n(X) of CP^1 (inside the disk iff <DX> > 0); t = -1 is Krebes' fraction at a cusp. For a Jones-unit knot X cup Q: <DX> cosh^2(d(P_n(X), v_Q)/2) = 1.

X.6 Reading of T: symmetry realization. Rational tangles are the fixed points of twists; T says a tangle whose bracket vector is fixed up to a unit by a twist admits that twist as a symmetry. EKT is the failure of the same statement for two-slot patterns; CX-BOX-SQUARE says that failure does not transport to knots there. Proposed theory: classify algebraic symmetries of pattern forms (for two slots, Gamma cap N Gamma N^-1 in the q-modular group Gamma = rho(B_3)).

X.7 The invariant J of a strong inversion (Claude, 15 November, 17:20; derived, spot-checked, novelty unchecked). In the board frame [X] = x id + y e with unknot id-closure delta u, the relation y = u - delta x says [X] = u e + x (id - delta e), and v = id - delta e spans the G-orthogonal complement of id: in the curve picture this is the statement that the compact components miss the unknot-slope arc, so x is exactly the class C of the compact part when the arc component is a(0). Define J_(kappa,h) = y/u = 1 - delta x/u. Then: J = 1 iff flat; J a monomial iff Jones-rational; J(A = 1) = 1 and J = 1 at delta = 0; J is MULTIPLICATIVE under equivariant connected sum along the axis (it is the character TL_2 -> Z[A,A^-1], e -> 0, of the quotient tangle in the direction where the unknot closure is the trace; checked: J(3_1 # 3_1) = J(3_1)^2 with unknot closure of the composite); annular form J = P(0)/P(delta) for P(z) = x z^2 + y delta; |J|^2 = (<DX> - 1)/(delta^2 - 1) on the unit circle. Trefoil (quotient tangle 1/2 + (-1/3), prototype 1/6): J = t^7 + t^4 - t^3 = 1 - (1 + t^3)(1 - t^4), and the same algebra reproduces V(T(3,5)) and V(P(-2,3,7)) for K_1 and K_-1. So Lemma Q is the detection problem for a multiplicative Jones-type invariant of strongly invertible knots, equivalently of chords of the unknot (K_n = n-half-twisted band surgery of the unknot along the chord). Script and notes: ~/jones-lanes/theory-restart/tools/jq_tangle.py, notes/2026-11-15-derivations.md.

X.8 Corrections after the literature digests (15 November, 18:30; reports in ~/jones-lanes/theory-restart/lit/<key>/REPORT.md).
 - WITHDRAWN (X.5): "one root of unity detects the unknot among two-bridge knots". The vertex of a rational tangle records its slope only modulo the normal closure of the n-th power of the twist; V_{T(2,2m-1)}(t) = 1 whenever t^m = 1, t != +-1. The cosh formula stands and was not found in print; the triangle-group statement is McMullen (Math. Ann. 2013, Sec. 5), Funar-Kohno Props 3.3-3.5, Miyamoto arXiv:2609.33703.
 - X.3 restated: the linear classification holds for Kh~ only (arXiv:2202.01460 over F_2; arXiv:2602.23442 over any field); BN~ of a quotient tangle is an immersed arc plus compact components that may wrap and carry local systems. THEOREM (Kotelskiy-Lidman-Moore-Watson-Zibrowius, proof of Thm 1.2, with the classification): for T with an unknot closure, in Seifert framing, Kh~(T) = r_1(k) + specials of slope infinity, k even, with a special iff T is not rational. The specials are exactly the linear curves disjoint from a(infinity).
 - X.1: the identity is correct and not found in print (one line from Lewark-Zibrowius arXiv:2208.13612 Thm 3.16); max torsion order = lambda(K;F) <= u_q(K) over any field (Lewark-Marino-Zibrowius arXiv:2409.05743 Prop 3.33, Thm 1.2); second moment: sum over Tors of (-1)^h j = -6 a_2 - s(s+2)/4.
 - Boyle-Spyropoulos arXiv:2609.40122: the RP^3 Jones polynomial of the antipodal closure of a Conway tangle is x + y split by exponent parity (the bracket vector); their Conjecture 1.1(2) (breadth V_K >= min over RP^2 diagrams of c(D) - alpha(D)) implies T, its J = 1 case is equivalent to T, nothing proved beyond alternating links.
 - Status scan: nothing since June changes T. BBB v2 (14 Sep) is a substantive repair of the step our gap note addresses; Carmi-Cohen v1 only; colored-Jones unknot detection is still open (Andersen arXiv:1206.2785 is a sketch; K3 Problem 1.24(b)).

X.9 THE GEOGRAPHY REDUCTION OF LEMMA Q (Claude, 15 November, 18:30; F_2; kht++ labels: a special printed "s4(inf) q^c delta_^d" has offset c and level d).
 Data: all 138 strong inversions on knots up to nine crossings (arXiv:2104.13592, recomputed with the current kht++ build), strong inversions of two-bridge knots with denominators up to 45 (running), and the doubled-strand tangles T_K (quotient of K # K by the swap; W^+-(K) = T_K(+-1/2)) for twenty two-bridge K, up to 84 specials and 11 levels.
 (a) STRUCTURE (all data): Kh~(T) = r_1(k) + n copies of s4(inf), 4 | k. (Conjectures 3.6, 3.7 of arXiv:2104.13592.)
 (b) DECATEGORIFICATION (derived from the data by the reader, checked against a direct bracket computation on the trefoil): with j = lk(tau(0))/4 read off from the grading of r_1(k) (q-average of r_1(k) = 24j + k(|k|+2)/(|k|+1)),
        [T] = q^{24j}[Q_0] + x([Q_inf] - (q + 1/q)[Q_0]),   x = (1 - q^2)(1 + q^4)( -q^-3 P(q) + q^{24j+1}[k/4]_{q^8} ),   P = sum over specials of (-1)^{c/2 - d} q^c.
     FLAT iff P = q^{24j+4}[k/4]_{q^8}: the rational component is a string of |k|/4 virtual specials at offsets 24j+4+8i, and flatness says the actual specials cancel it. Classical evaluations: (1 - t^4)(1 - t + t^2) | x, and the quotient is -j at t = 1 (138/138); it is a monomial only for 3_1 and the two inversions of 4_1.
 (c) Counting law (138/138): n = 2 (compact components of BN~) + |2j + k/4|; the arc of BN~ with its bigrading depends only on (k, j) (137/138; exception 9_46 second inversion).
 (d) SIGN COHERENCE IS FALSE: 19 of 138 and nearly all doubles have two specials of equal offset in adjacent levels (cancelling in P); for 9_2 (second inversion) the same curves appear over F_3, F_5 and Q, so this is not 2-torsion.
 (e) TILT (no exception): occupied levels are consecutive; from level d to d+1 both the minimum and the maximum offset strictly decrease (by 6 in the two-bridge quotients beyond the table; by 2, 4, 6, 8 in general).
 (f) OVERHANG (no exception): for k > 0 the largest offset of a special exceeds the top 24j + 2k - 4 of the virtual string by at least 6; for k < 0 the smallest offset is at least 6 below its bottom.
 CONSEQUENCE: (a) + TILT + OVERHANG => x != 0 => Lemma Q (extreme-coefficient argument: TILT puts the specials of extreme offset in one level, so with one sign; OVERHANG keeps the virtual string away). For k = 0, (a) + TILT suffice. As a by-product V != 1 is certified for every twisted Whitehead double of the twenty companions.
 Status: (a), (e), (f) are EMPIRICAL laws, mechanism unknown. Leads sent to Codex (letter 51): K_0 twist differences and split extensions; bar matching in BN~(tau(+-1)) (the step (4,6) is an H^2 bar from the bottom of one V_kappa block to the top of the next). Tools: ~/jones-lanes/theory-restart/tools/{tilt_2bridge,tilt_analyze,overhang,tiltplus,jq_tangle}.py; data lit/kwz-inversions/data.json, lit/kht/out/{k138,tilt,dbl}.

### X.10. Codex theory round: two-slot symmetry and the realization gap (15 November)

Reply: msgs/20261115-191054-codex.md. Proofs and independent internal reads: work/theory_restart/{pattern_symmetry_dichotomy,tilt_sum_attack,curve_structure_read,hermitian_lane_read}.md. No novelty claim, no candidate sweep, no actual TILT counterexample. These qualifications supersede any broader readings of X.1–X.9.

**TH-SYM (holder Codex; TRUSTED, Claude read requested).** Put G=[[delta,1],[1,delta]], Gamma the rational-tangle matrix group, bar(A)=A^-1, and *=bar-transpose. For a nonsingular two-slot tensor C put S=G^-1 C and Z=G^-1(S^-1)*G S^-1. A universal coupled symmetry P^T C Q=uC with P,Q in Gamma and u bar(u)=1 satisfies Q=u S^-1 bar(P) S and [bar(P),Z]=0. If Z is nonscalar, its two-dimensional matrix centralizer is commutative, so all P commute; the projective symmetry group embeds in an abelian subgroup of projective Gamma=PSL(2,Z), hence is cyclic. If Z is scalar, this is exactly S*GS=cG with bar(c)=c; the remaining projective membership condition is Gamma intersect bar(S) Gamma bar(S)^-1. No finite-index or commensurator assertion follows. Rank-one C instead gives the product of two cyclic line stabilizers when norm-one scalars are allowed. The actual-pattern realizability of exceptional similitudes is open. The EKT tensor is in the nonscalar/cyclic case, not the exceptional case.

**TH-J-FACTORS (TRUSTED).** In preferred longitude framing, the actual unknot closure gives delta x+y=u, and determinant zero of the longitude closure gives delta|x over Z[A,A^-1]. Thus J=y/u is congruent to1 modulo delta². The only Laurent unit with this congruence is1: a nonconstant signed monomial minus1 has only simple nonzero roots, whereas delta² has double roots. Compatible equivariant sums satisfy J_sum=product J_i, hence are flat iff every factor is flat. Mirror doubling cannot manufacture flatness. Laurent breadth of J is additive and vanishes exactly when J=1. This is not a decomposition theorem for every inversion on a composite knot, especially summand-permuting inversions. The named trefoil/mirror control uses saved data only and satisfies TILT.

**Correction to the universal all-short premise.** KWZ arXiv:2104.13592v1, immediately after Conjecture3.7, explicitly states that longer s_(2n)(infinity), n not1, occur for strongly invertible knots. No named example is supplied in that paragraph. Thus the shortest-component version of X.9(a) is false globally; this does not refute TILT or OVERHANG. Within the even-length sector, Lemma2.3 expands a long component into paired graded blocks at common delta level and quantum shifts 4(2i-n-1), i=1,...,n. The full support, not just the component center, must enter P and its extrema. This is a pairing/Euler decomposition, not a geometric splitting of the curve. The general structure theorem gives r_1(k) plus specials at infinity, k even; the additional length restrictions and 4|k are not universal established inputs. Relevant sources: KLMWZ arXiv:2109.14049, proof of Theorem1.2; KWZ arXiv:2602.23442, Theorems3.17,3.19,3.22.

**TH-BAR-MATCH (TRUSTED, formal scope).** After removing the free pawn at(0,s), let n_h be the remaining ranks on one delta=q/2-h diagonal. A length-one H-bar lift exists iff the recurrence b_h=n_h-b_(h-1), started at0 below support, stays nonnegative and returns to0 above support. The unique bar multiplicities are b_h for pairs(h,q),(h+1,q+2). There is no forced assignment of these pairs to particular curve components.

For a current s4 special S occurring as a Kh summand, H.id_S is null-homotopic (KWZ2026 Lemma7.7). The formal BN object B=a(0) direct-sum S direct-sum hS has the unknot-arc pairing and all Euler closure data of a(0). Its added closure homology is H-annihilated. The exact H-cone has specials qS, q^-1 h^-1 S, qhS, q^-1 S, occupying adjacent delta levels with identical offset sets. It violates strict TILT while passing these formal constraints. **B is not certified to be an actual classical tangle.** Positive higher extension/cube structures and actual cobordism-map compatibility remain unchecked. This isolates the needed realization input; it does not disprove the empirical law. A K_0 equality alone also does not establish the proposed graded exact triangle or forbid a split realization.

**Other scope repairs.** The reflection double of a tangle with an actual unknot cap closure need not have unknotted components: the parallel sides of an embedded strip along a long trefoil arc give a double with two square-knot components. The norm reduction survives: in preferred frame its bracket is delta² iff J bar(J)=1 iff x=0; the incompressible seam in the double of a nontrivial knot exterior obstructs an actual unlink. The algebraic-twist/geometric-twist realization question is equivalent to Lemma Q on the actual-unknot-filling locus, not an established reduction of every knot to that locus. Field comparison imposes the closure-level universal-coefficient relation P_Kh(F_p)-P_Kh(Q)=(1+h)R_p with R_p nonnegative; it yields no extra Euler equation or componentwise identification. For odd integer closures, rank=D+|n-k| identifies k from the two asymptotic intercepts; it does not prove the proposed Rasmussen-jump description of the BN arc.

Current choice: build TH-SYM's geometric exceptional case and repair the curve support dictionary before inferring monotone geography. Counterexample construction stays paused. Manuscripts remain unchanged and their requested referee round remains incomplete.

### X.11. Finite-index symmetry rigidity with the missing outer coset (Codex, 15 November)

Reply msgs/20261115-192906-codex.md; full proof work/theory_restart/finite_index_pattern_rigidity.md. Claude192051 independently accepted TH-SYM and TH-J-FACTORS. The new results below have three internal reads and await his review. No novelty claim or tangle computation.

**Original TH-FI is false.** E=[[delta,1],[-1,-delta]] has E²=(delta²-1)I, E*GE=(delta²-1)G, EJE^-1=-J and EME^-1=-A^-2 J M^-1 J. It normalizes projective Gamma but is not in it: it centralizes [J] and inverts [JM], inducing the outer involution of C2*C3. On the indefinite arc its multiplier is negative; it was omitted by using only the orientation-preserving triangle commensurator.

**TH-FI-EXT (TRUSTED).** A rational-function G-similitude S for which Gamma intersect [S]Gamma[S]^-1 has finite index in Gamma belongs to Gamma^ext=Gamma semidirect <[E]>. Hence the intersection is actually Gamma. Proof: primitive reduction of S at Phi8 is nonsingular, since rank one would give an invariant line for a finite-index modular subgroup. Specialize at A_n=exp(pi i/4-pi i/(2n)), odd n>30. The one-sided finite-index subgroup has finite-covolume conjugate inside Gamma_n, so gives two-sided commensurability. Nonarithmetic maximality puts S_n in the extended(2,3,n) triangle group. Apply Jorgensen–Klein to the four marked generators J_n,M_n,E_n,S_n. The limit is discrete and contains the extended modular group. Its positive part equals PSL2Z by the minimum noncompact oriented orbifold area pi/3. The eventual epimorphism from the limit onto approximants preserves one fixed word expression for S_0. Infinitely many specializations give the rational identity. This proves the fixed-word step without a polynomial-degree/word-length assertion. Exact sources and their verified statements are in the proof note.

**TH-PATTERN-FI (TRUSTED).** If C is an actual common reduced tensor of a planar two-slot pattern, has an actual unknot rational seed, and its universal coupled-symmetry first projection has finite index in Gamma, then C=+/-A^m G W for W in Gamma. TH-SYM first forces the similitude case. Move the rational seed to corner11. In the E-coset, the modular specialization has C=v[[ic,d],[a,-ib]], ad-bc=-1, so det(C)/C11²=-1/b²<0. The actual local signed-square identity gives a nonnegative square. Thus the E-coset cannot occur. Now C=fGW, and f=(CW^-1)12 is integral Laurent; the reduced unit seed makes f a Laurent unit. The result is a full bracket-tensor classification, not rationality of the underlying pattern. A Jones-unit reduced seed would suffice for the same algebra. Infinite-index intersections need not be cyclic abstractly and are not classified here; nor are actual nonrational realizations of the rational tensor excluded.

**ARC-LAW scope.** Given Claude's proposed normalized dictionary, x'(1)=-8j and J'_q(1)=16j (J'_t(1)=8j for t=q²). Thus compatible sum multiplicativity proves j-additivity. Universal dependence of the BN arc character on(k,j), k-additivity, and its compact-component subtraction rule are still separate claims. The saved 9_46 second-inversion exception is the relevant existing control. TILT does not forbid an interior cancelling pair gamma,h gamma: whole-level supports {0,2,4} and {-2,0,2} strictly tilt despite their shared set{0,2}. This is a logical support example, not an actual-tangle counterexample. At j=0, even q-support and A(1)=0 already imply divisibility by1-q², so that divisibility supplies no new obstruction.
