### Referee-amendment handoff, Codex200352 (completed at the pause)

Claude195721's three reports were read and the proof revised at work/theory_restart/finite_index_pattern_rigidity.md. No referee scans were replayed. Two short deductions were checked internally: (1) the finite-index hypothesis on arbitrary S already implies similitude, since hS=alpha Sk forces alpha=+/-A^m, so H preserves both G and (S^-1)*G S^-1; a nonscalar quotient of these forms would have commutative centralizer, contradicting nonabelian finite-index H. Thus TH-FI-EXT applies to every rational S and identifies the full normalizer without an Aut(PSL2Z) input. (2) The tessellation proof is primary: choose x in int(T0) with gx off the countably many limiting walls, write gx in h0 int(T0), and use convergence to put x and h_n^-1g_nx in int(Tn). Strict chamber interiors force g_n=h_n eventually. The negative-multiplier disk identification is continuous. Jorgensen–Klein/coarea remains an alternative, with the original-paper access limitation stated.

The referee's A=1 exclusion is incorporated without enumeration: K=M(1)J satisfies K²=K-I and K³=-I; every group element is +/-I, +/-K, +/-K² or a row swap, hence has a zero entry. A nonzero entry first makes the outer scalar regular at1; a zero entry would make an actual link bracket vanish there. Therefore TH-PATTERN-FI gives C=fGW, f integral Laurent, with no seed. Only the further conclusion f=+/-A^m needs a unit rational seed. The Hopf-summand tensor (-A4-A^-4)G shows that requirement cannot be removed. The scalar f is itself a rational-input closure bracket, not a claimed topological summand. Signed square is retained at every nonzero zeta8 corner, with explicit 0/1 labels and the connectivity correction.

These amendments were the already-underway work when the pause notice was read. No new research, mechanism-report review, scan or manuscript work was begun. Reply: msgs/20261115-200352-codex.md. On resumption, Claude can check the two short additions; X.13's sum/maps reports remain UNREFEREED.


## X.14. Codex referee of sum and maps reports after Caleb resumed theory

The direct user request resumed work without Claude. No bridge message or new knot search was made. The scoped referee synthesis is `work/theory_restart/jtilt_referee_synthesis.md`; independent reads are `sum_functor_referee.md`, `jtilt_implication_referee.md`, and `maps_report_referee.md` in the same directory. These supersede the UNREFEREED label on the corresponding claims in the historical pause snapshot above, not every assertion of those reports.

**TH-BULLET-SUM, TRUSTED.** The augmentation sending the horizontal unit matching b to F2, the other matching to zero, and nonidentity paths to zero defines a homotopy-invariant bigraded space N_b(T). Only (b,b) yields b under the product; delooping creates only the other idempotent. Augmented complexes tensor, so N_b(T1+T2)=N_b(T1) tensor N_b(T2), including subsequent reduction. Its integer Euler characteristic is the bracket coordinate uJ.

**TH-BULLET-RANK, TRUSTED, independently checked twice.** On T(infinity)=U, dim N_b(T)=1+2m, m the number of special components with multiplicity, regardless of lengths. Current KWZ2602.23442 Def3.12 gives four b-endpoint generators of every special at infinity; the interior generators lie in the other idempotent. The rational r1(k) has two; the H-cone doubles the augmentation. Published structure and rationality detection give rank one iff rational. Consequently 1+2m is multiplicative under compatible sum, and a prime value prevents two nonrational factors. This is not underlying-knot primality. The detailed graded special-tensor formula still has its separate finite tensor input; the universal count theorem does not depend on it.

**END reduction, TRUSTED conditional theorem; universal END OPEN.** END means nonzero integer Euler sums at both unsigned quantum endpoints of N_b. J-TILT implies endpoint parity purity, which implies END. Any actual nonrational quotient tangle has positive unsigned quantum breadth: a length-ell special has endpoint breadth 2ell+2 in the H-cone, while the cone increases the breadth of N_b by exactly2. Thus END makes uJ nonmonomial, proving nonflatness. Universal END on this locus implies Lemma Q. This removes all-short-special, 4|k, empirical centering and OVERHANG assumptions. END is a stronger-than-necessary geography conjecture, not equivalent to Lemma Q. Endpoint cancellation losses are additive under sums; END and parity purity hold for a product iff both factors satisfy them. A minimal failure can be placed in a factor indecomposable under this specific compatible equivariant sum; summand-permuting inversions are not covered. No input proving END for such factors has been found in these reports.

**TH-MAPS-ZERO, TRUSTED with scope corrections.** The three explicit homotopies for D_b, D_c and S^2 on the 12-generator short special were checked independently by `work/theory_restart/referee_checks/maps_exact.py`; the full 16-dimensional End table agrees. Existential central-composite map pairs extend by zero when shifted specials are added. This permits formal B=a0+S+hS and cannot establish END/J-TILT. It says nothing decisive about prescribed geometric maps, compatibility among several maps/homotopies, arbitrary deletion, or integral refinements. The noncentral window includes equal-offset adjacent-level pairs; it neither forces a connecting map nor strict tilt. The saved b35_26-1 chain contains that pair among other components, so forbidding it locally is incompatible with the archive. Uniform closure-range and finer orientation assertions remain pending their omitted general proofs.

**Research decision.** Keep J-TILT as a stronger open hypothesis; use intrinsic endpoint visibility as the economical sufficient target. The reports give an actual structural reduction and a decomposition obstruction, but do not cross the global realizability gap. No new mechanism, actual counterexample, novelty claim, or conclusion for full Jones unknot detection is asserted.

## X.15. Claude after resumption (15 November, 22:00 to 23:15): N(T) is the Khovanov side of knot Floer homology; shape law; polygon law (letter 56)

Notes: ~/jones-lanes/theory-restart/notes/2026-11-15b-sutured-side.md. Tools: ~/jones-lanes/theory-restart/tools/{gtilde,grid,hull,alex,kauffman,alex_search,run_words}.py. Codex's X.14 is accepted with its scope; her two additions of 200352 are accepted (the first checked line by line).

**THEOREM S (PROVED given Grigsby-Wehrli; source statement to be pinned by Codex).** N(T) = top annular weight of AKh(T(n)) = Grigsby-Wehrli's Khovanov homology of the balanced tangle T in D x I. The double cover of D x I branched along T is the exterior of K with two meridional sutures, so there is a spectral sequence from N(mirror T) to HFK^(K; F_2). Hence 1 + 2m(T) >= rank HFK^(K) >= 2g(K) + 1 and m(T) >= g(K). Curve form: N(T) = HF(BN~(T), loop at the special puncture around p1), and that loop lifts to the meridian arc through the basepoint, as in the Hanselman-Rasmussen-Watson formula for HFK^. DATA: 1 + 2m = rank HFK^(K) in 131 of 132 table cases (8_19 and 9_42 included; exception 9_46 second inversion, 15 against 9) and = det(K)^2 for all 21 doubles.

**Representation form (PROVED).** In the horizontal direction T has eigenvalue u on V_0 and uJ on V_2. For the swap inversion on K # K, J is the reduced Jones polynomial coloured by the 3-dimensional representation (checked against Habiro's formula on 4_1). So Lemma Q contains "the 3-dimensional coloured Jones polynomial detects the unknot".

**Strip picture and SHAPE LAW (DATA; proof proposed to Codex).** In the planar cover every component lies in the strip around the special line of slope infinity; moves between consecutive b-generators are D^a, (h,q) = (1,2a), and P_n = S(DS^2)^(n-1)DS, (h,q) = (2n+1,4n). In all saved chain files the moves are D and P_2 (one D^2, in 9_46 second inversion). All 1420 compact components are the square; 548 of 551 arcs are pure staircases (the other three are invalid random words). So N = Z_l + Lambda^r over Lambda = F[d_D,d_P]/(d_D^2,d_P^2), 2l+1 = 2|2j + k/4| + 1, H(d_D) = H(d_P) = F: the structure of CFK of thin knots.

**Lattice coordinates.** b-generator = rho + aD + bP, D = (1,2), P = (5,8): h = a+5b, q = 2a+8b, delta = -b; J = sum of (-t)^a(-t^4)^b; J = chi(zigzag) + (1-t)(1-t^4) Pi, Pi = sum over squares of (-1)^(a+b) t^(a+4b). Cancellation: (a,b) against (a-4m, b+m), m odd. B = two squares offset by (-4,1) = (h,q) = (1,0). That offset occurs 333 times between squares inside actual tangles, so the missing law is global.

**POLYGON LAW (DATA, 283 distinct tangles).** Newton polygon of N in the (a,b) plane: edge directions (1,0) 468, (0,1) 424, (1,-1) 511, (2,-1) 177, (3,-2) 7, (1,1) 1, (7,-3) 1. In the (q/2, delta) plane each non-horizontal edge moves q/2 by 2, 3 or 4 per level (rarely 5/3, 5/2, 5); a cancellation at an extreme of J needs 0. Target **NP**: max q and min q on N(T) are each attained on one delta level. NP => EXT => END => Lemma Q.

**Alexander polynomial one test (DATA).** Search of 320,000 half-diagram words: two classes with Delta = 1 and non-trivial Jones polynomial. Class A (s.r1.y0.x2.r3.y0.y2.u1.x2.x2.y0.y1.x0.u1, four diagrams): r_1(+-4) + 15 specials, j = 0, dim N = 31 = 3-staircase + 7 squares, END holds, hull directions (1,0), (0,1), (1,-1) only, the seven squares at seven q-positions with no cancellation. On the Floer side such a knot has boxes of both parities that cancel; the quantum grading separates them. Class B running; a larger search running.

**Corrections.** In run_3_45.jsonl about half the two-bridge files produced by the generator are diagrams of smaller two-bridge knots (label wrong, tangle valid); to be relabelled before any statement indexed by p/q is made from that file.

Status: THEOREM S and the representation form PROVED given the cited theorem; SHAPE LAW, POLYGON LAW, NP are DATA/CONJ; no mechanism for NP.

So most special generators cancel, and under the degree rule every cancelling pair joins level delta to delta - 1 with q rising by 2r - 2 >= 2. (a) If R = 0 on the relevant side every special generator is matched: tilt at the top two and bottom two levels, no anti-tilt, bare B impossible. (b) In general the minimal rank after any such matching is at most the Floer rank: the tilt defect at the ends is bounded by R. ## X.17. MATCH: Daemi's plane Floer spectral sequence, and the first exclusion of bare B (Claude, 15 November, 23:50; letter 59)

Source read: Daemi, "Abelian gauge theory, knots and odd Khovanov homology", arXiv:1508.07650 (PDF and text in ~/jones-lanes/theory-restart/lit/daemi/). Theorem 2: PKC(D) is a link invariant as a filtered Z-graded complex; the differential does not decrease the homological grading and raises the delta-grading by 1. Theorems 3 and 4.9: the second page is the reduced odd Khovanov homology of the mirror (over his characteristic two field: the usual reduced Khovanov homology), bigraded, with deg_q = 2(deg_h - deg_delta) + 1. The total homology is PFH of the branched double cover, and PFH(Y) = H_*(R(Y)), R(Y) = |Tor H_1(Y)| copies of a torus of dimension b_1(Y) (his subsection 1.4).

**MATCH (PROVED given Daemi).** For every link L with det(L) != 0, Kh~(L; F_2) is the second page of a spectral sequence with r-th differential of bidegree exactly (r, 2r - 2) and limit of rank det(L). Hence the generators of Kh~(L) can be paired, each pair joining delta-level d to d - 1 with q rising by at least 2, leaving exactly det(L) generators. (For det = 0 and b_1 = 1 the limit has rank 2.) The degree rule needs no input from Szabo's or the Ozsvath-Szabo sequence.

**Bare B is not a tangle (PROVED given MATCH and the pairing theorem).** For a(0) + S + hS the knots K_n = T(1/n) have determinant 1 and Kh~(K_n) = one generator + the strings of S on one level + the same strings one level down. The largest admissible pairing leaves 2n + 1 generators (3, 5, 7 for n = 1, 2, 3; tools/daemi_match.py). This is the first exclusion of the formal counterexample by a structure of actual tangles: the plane Floer complexes of the branched double covers of the closures.

**Not excluded: ladders.** Q_1 + hQ_1 + Q_2 + hQ_2 passes MATCH at K_n (n = 1..4) exactly when Q_2 lies one level above Q_1 and lower in q by any positive amount (or the mirror); longer ladders pass too. Example in lattice coordinates: squares at (0,0), (-4,1), (1,-1), (-3,0). A ladder is flat, satisfies SHAPE and both collapses, and fails TILT only at its top and bottom rows. Ladders are the formal objects still to be excluded.

**Data.** MATCH holds (as it must) on every computed closure: 3_1, 4_1, 5_2 (both), 8_19, 9_42, 9_46 (both), n = 0, +-1, ..., +-4: the largest pairing leaves exactly |n|. Torus knots T(3,4), T(3,5), T(3,7), T(3,8), T(3,10), T(4,5), T(4,7), T(5,6): matchable to the determinant.
**Alexander polynomial one, continued.** Class C (s.l1.y0.y2.r3.x2.y1.x0.y2.y2.u1.x0.x2.x0.u1): k = j = 0, arc a(0), dim N = 17 = point + four squares at (-1,1), (0,0), (0,0), (1,-1), Pi = t^3 + 2 + t^-3; class B (s.r1.x2.x0.x0.x1.x2.x2.x1.y0.x1.y0.u1): k = j = 0, dim N = 65 = point + 16 squares. END holds in both. Parity note: the row (delta) parity, not the h parity, passes to the Floer side.

Status: MATCH and "bare B is not a tangle" PROVED given the cited theorems; ladders OPEN; NP OPEN.


## X.17. Plane-Floer matching and the natural ladder (Codex234738)

This supersedes the conditional/unlabelled matching interpretation of X.16, not the actual NP conjecture. Three independent source/algebra reads are in work/theory_restart/{surgery_collapse_sources_referee,surgery_ladder_referee,low_rank_hfk_referee}.md; synthesis surgery_hall_reduction.md. Claude233905's withdrawal of the rank-five classification was read and accepted. No Codex sweep.

**TH-PFH-GRADES.** Daemi1508.07650, author version2January2017, §1.3, Lemma3.10, Cor3.15, Thm4.9 already gives d_r degree(r,2r-2) on reduced Kh of the mirror over a characteristic-two Novikov field: q=2(h-delta_Daemi)+1 and delta_Daemi rises1. PFH rank=2^b1|Tor H1|. Thus quantum monotonicity is not an open refinement for this sequence; its degree rule is not automatically that of the OS Heegaard Floer sequence.

**TH-HALL, TRUSTED.** Form the graph on graded multiplicities with edges of degree(r,2r-2),r>=2. It is bipartite by delta parity. With mu=dim-2max_matching, actual graded spectral-sequence cancellations give a matching; conversely a matching makes only a formal filtered differential. Delta Euler characteristic gives mu>=det, and Daemi gives mu<=det for nonzero determinant, hence equality. Equivalently the smaller parity part satisfies Hall's inequalities. This rejects formal unrealizable closure profiles; it does not turn actual-link matching experiments into new evidence for NP.

For T(n), mu=|n| when n!=0 and mu<=2 at n=0. The inequality mu<=rank HFhat(S3_n(kappa)) is therefore automatic from standard HF rank bounds; it gives no extra R constraint. OS journal Prop9.6 requires p!=0,q>0,nu(K)>=nu(mK), has separate sign cases, and does not cover zero surgery. The rational closure contribution at n=k is2, not0. Target rank alone does not say all special generators die, because the rational part has |n-k| generators and later maps may mix summands. Complete monotone matching alone also does not imply TILT of both extrema at every level.

**TH-LADDER, TRUSTED formal certificate.** Q is the compact BN object with bullet square at0,(1,2),(5,8),(6,10). Let L2=a0+Q+hQ+h^-8 q^-14 Q+h^-7 q^-14 Q. The identities from shifted copies to the unshifted copies have degree(8,14), square0 and commute with the original differential overF2. They cancel all four compact summands under every additive graded closure pairing, leaving the rational closure (rank|n| for n!=0, rank2 at0). They also commute with filling-side maps, central actions and square maps; the unreduced lift persists. Yet N has rank17,Euler1, and both endpoints cancel: q=-14 at h=-8,-7 and q10 at h6,7. Thus w_-=w_+=1. This is not an actual tangle, covering knot or geometric Floer differential. It shows formal all-closure naturality alone is not the missing realization constraint. The exact grading shifts are essential.

**TH-S-LOWRANK, TRUSTED.** TH-S plus HW1404.6913 Cor8 and a2=0 gives dimN>=5 for flat nonrationalT. If equality, rankHFK5 and collapse. Writing Delta=a0+sum ai(t^i+t^-i), ||Delta||1<=5 and odda0 give sum|ai|<=2; a2=sum ai i²=0 forces allai=0. Therefore Delta1. Genus detection/conjugation give ranks(2,1,2) at(-g,0,g), no others, nonfibered. The hat canceling differential cannot leave the central generator: two degree-minus-one top-to-bottom pairs and the conjugation shift2g would force4g=2. Thus |tau|=g. This exact residual profile is not classified or ruled out. Strong-inversion enumeration is unnecessary because a2 excludes the named small knots independently of inversion. The stronger dimN>=7 is unproved, as already corrected233905.

**Class-A certificate.** Direct polynomial Bareiss on the existing14-crossing diagram gives the13-by-13 cofactor -t^7, normalizedDelta1, agreeing with Claude's exact interpolation. work/theory_restart/referee_checks/alex_class_a_direct.py reuses the diagram/matrix conventions but not interpolation. No new candidates computed.


## X.18. Bare B excluded by one closure; the strip lemma (Codex235614)

Claude234649 and235059 crossed reply234738. MATCH is the common Daemi reading, precisely over the characteristic-two Novikov field; scalar extension preserves the F2 graded dimensions, so the Hall conclusion applies as stated. Claude235059 independently accepted TH-LADDER and TH-S-LOWRANK; those formal/restricted results are now PROVED with their existing scopes.

**TH-B-EXCLUSION / TH-MATCH-STRIP, TRUSTED.** The entire12-generator compact BN componentQ, H-coned and paired with a(1)=[c(-1,-2)->S b(0,-1)], has exact Hom homology (h,q)=0,(1,2),(2,4),2(3,6),(4,8),(5,10),(6,12). The independent script work/theory_restart/referee_checks/bare_b_slope_one.py checks grading, square-zero, exhaustive stable tails and an explicit seven-pair matching. This is a full closure calculation, not inferred from the four bullet generators. KWZ1910.14584v2 Prop4.31/Thm7.2 supplies the gluing interpretation.

B=a0+Q+hQ therefore has a pawn and two identical rows M={0,1,2,3,3,4,5,6} in p=q/2, at adjacent delta levels. Strictly increasing pairs leave at least3 generators: the upper tail>=3 has5 sources but only3 targets>3, and lower0 is stranded. Seven admissible pairs attain3. This contradicts MATCH at a determinant-one knot closure, so bareB is unrealizable. Changing the pawn or overallQ shift cannot remove the obstruction.

General Hall formula: two identical ordered multisetsM with strict-rise edges have max_matching=|M|-w, w=max position multiplicity. A tail atp has deficiency multiplicity(p), and no subset has more than its full tail. Adding one pawn permits at most one extra pair, so residual>=2w-1. Hence all a0+sum(Q_i+hQ_i), with theQ_i on one common closure delta level and arbitrary quantum shifts, are excluded: each translatedQ row already hasw>=2. This is a genuine restriction on realizable configurations, not just a rejection of one sample.

**Count correction.** Conditional on Claude's uniform row polynomial (1+z)(1+z²)(1+z³)[n]_z, max multiplicity ismin(n+1,8), so optimal arbitrary-pawn residual is2min(n+1,8)-1. It agrees with2n+1 only throughn7 and then stays15; n8is15not17. The n1 direct pairing already proves exclusion, so no all-n formula or additional test is needed.

**Ladders.** Claude235059 extends the identity certificate to every positive gapg: tau_g=h^(-g-1)q^(-2g), mapsdegree(g+1,2g), smallestpage2. These models have three compact closure delta rows and evade the strip lemma; both unsigned endpoints still cancel. They remain the formal test objects for NP/END, with no classical realization or identification with geometric Floer maps claimed. ActualNP/END remainOPEN.

**X.17 addendum (00:05, 16 November): where bare-B structure does occur, and why not for us.** The simplest EKT link LL2(1) (closure of sigma_3^3 (sigma_2^-1 sigma_3^-1 sigma_1^-1 sigma_2^-1)^2 sigma_1^-3, Jones polynomial a nontrivial monomial times that of the two-component unlink (normalization corrected Codex001802); braid from Watson's thesis via work/ekt_homology_notes.md) computed with kht++ over F_2: rank Kh~ = 26 on two adjacent delta-levels, 13 generators each, and Kh~ = (the two unlink generators) + X + hX with X of rank 12: the second level is the first shifted by (h,q) = (1,0) except for one generator at each of two q-values. So the structure of bare B (X + hX, same q, adjacent delta) is realised by an actual LINK. Daemi's bound explains it: H_1 of the double cover is Z + Z/13 (Codex's notes), so the limit of the plane Floer sequence has rank 2 x 13 = 26 and the sequence collapses; the invisible classes are permanent. General consequence of Daemi (PROVED given the source): rank Kh~(L;F_2) >= 2^{b_1} |Tor H_1| of the double cover, with equality for LL2(1). For closures of a quotient tangle, H_1 of the double cover is cyclic of order |p|, so there is no excess target rank: the excess source dimension must cancel in Daemi's sequence. This does not canonically identify which generators die (scope corrected Codex001802). So for us the only possible flat structures are the cancelling ones (ladders); the EKT mechanism cannot occur. For a KNOT with trivial Jones polynomial the same holds: Kh~ = one generator + a part that cancels between adjacent delta-levels with q strictly increasing; if Kh~ lies on two adjacent levels, the invisible part X must be multiplicity-free in q and lie entirely on one side of q = 0.

**X.18 (Codex 235614, read 00:10; accepted).** TH-B-EXCLUSION: Codex computed the slope-one pairing of the full H-cone of the compact component Q exactly and independently: eight generators on one delta-level at positions q/2 in M = {0,1,2,3,3,4,5,6}; for B this gives the pawn and two copies of M on adjacent levels, the largest admissible matching has 7 pairs, residual exactly 3 against determinant 1. TH-MATCH-STRIP (Hall): two identical copies of a multiset M on adjacent levels have maximum matching |M| - w, w the largest multiplicity; with one extra generator at least 2w - 1 survive. So a(0) + sum_i (Q_i + hQ_i) is not a tangle whenever all the Q_i pair on one delta-level, for arbitrary quantum offsets. Correction to X.17: the residual of bare B at K_n is 2 min(n+1, 8) - 1 (15 from n = 7 on), not 2n + 1 for all n; the exclusion needs only n = 1. Coefficient precision: Daemi's sequence is over his characteristic two Novikov field; the matching consequence is a statement about F_2 graded dimensions. TH-LADDER: every gap g >= 1 gives a formal certificate L_g = a(0) + Q + hQ + tau_g Q + h tau_g Q with identity-block differential of degree (g+1, 2g) (page two for g = 1), natural under all closures; NP and END fail for it. A mechanism must explain why the extra row with its identity-block cancellation cannot arise geometrically.
Data added by Claude: the Alexander-polynomial-one knot of class A passes MATCH at n = 0, +-1, ..., +-4 (ranks 61 to 68, residual |n|).

**TH-S-GENUS1, TRUSTED.** A genus-one Delta1 knot has positive even rational top HFK rank. Rank2 is excluded by Baldwin–Sivek, Forum Math. Pi13(2025)e1, Thm1.2/Table1: all genus-one nearly fibered knots have determinant7 or9. Thus the top and bottom ranks are at least4 and the middle at least1; total rational and F2 ranks are at least9 by UCT. Flatness gives a2=0, hence Delta1 in genus1; TH-S then gives dimN>=9, m>=4. This eliminates genus1 at dimN5 and7 but does not prove universal dimN>=7.

Its rational top rank is exactly2, so Li–Ye2208.05382v3 Thm1.4/Cor1.5/Remark2.1 force guts M1, a solid torus with four longitudinal sutures: the other models have nonzero top Alexander coefficient. The residual has HFK(g-1)=0. Ni's next-to-top theorem requires single top parity (fails), and Binns–Wan2608.03900v1 Thm1.3 requires a particular two-annulus decomposition (not supplied). Their Remark4.13 explicitly leaves arbitrary annular/disc decompositions open. No low-rank classification is being inferred beyond these precise restrictions.

**TH-FIELD-END, TRUSTED.** Integral bullet augmentation is finite free. If t_p(h,q) counts p-primary cyclic integral homology summands, dimN_Fp(h,q)=dimN_Q(h,q)+t_p(h,q)+t_p(h+1,q). Rational support is contained in mod-p support and integer Euler polynomials agree. END_Fp implies END_Q because visible endpoints must occur in rational support; NP_Fp implies NP_Q by the same argument and endpoint concentration. Outside finitely many torsion primes the dimension profiles agree. Hence for P=END orNP, P_Q iff P_Fp for somep iff P_Fp for all but finitely manyp. Exceptional primes depend onT. This compares endpoint statements, not curve decompositions, and supplies no noncancellation by itself.

**TH-LADDER-Z, TRUSTED formal certificate.** Give compactQ's edge1->12 (Db) coefficient-1 and all other edges+1. The signed Q^- has termwise integral d²0, free bullet rank4, integral cap acyclicity and null-homotopies forDb,Dc,S²,H=D-S². All-plusQ does not have the same characteristic-zero central null-homotopies. For tau=h^-3q^-4, L=a0+Q^-+hQ^-+tauQ^-+h tauQ^- has identities ofdegree(3,4), anticommuting with the original differential by odd-shift signs. Additive closures retain the formal collapse. Nrank17/Euler1 and endpointsq=-4,10 cancel overQ,C andeveryFp. Independent root rerun of referee_checks/integral_ladder.py passed. No classical tangle, geometric Floer differential, general integral curve classification or extension of the F2 unreduced-dot algebra is claimed.

**TH-INSTANTON, target correction.** Xie1809.01568v2 Cor1.2/Thm6.1 gives N_C(mirrorT)=>THI(T) via top annular weight, no extraV factor. THI is the original-cylinder singular tangle invariant, not SHI of the branched cover. Doubled cut-open trefoil gives THI=I-natural(trefoil), rank<=3 by XieThm6.3, whereas KHI of the cover knot trefoil#trefoil has rank>=||Delta²||1=9. Thus the proposed target identification is REFUTED. Rank/braid detection survive; importing covering-knot instanton low-rank classifications does not. New information about actual instanton maps could still matter.

Matching residual1 iffM is multiplicity-free and allp>0 when pawn upper, or allp<0 when pawn lower. For upperpawn, matching all lower prefixes gives |M_<=p|<=|M_<p|+1, hence multiplicity<=1; the minimum forcesp_min>0. Conversely ordered chain0->p1->...->pm matches the smaller row. Its survivor is upperpm, so even the pawn can die. Reverse for lowerpawn. This is an exact abstract matching criterion and a necessary restriction on actual two-row Jones-unit homology. It neither proves realizability nor addresses the multilevel ladders. NP/END remainOPEN.

 - Topology: T is a two-string string link with strands the half-axes A_u, A_l; T(0) = K_u u K_l (constituent knots of the theta-curve); N(T) = top annular weight of this link with respect to the meridian of the band along kappa. Two-bridge quotients are not sums of rational tangles; they are "axis + band wound by a 3-braid", words in Y (axis crossing) and X (off-axis crossing); K_l is unknotted, K_u = K(p/(q/2)).
 - THEOREM A: for side-preserving two-bridge inversions, J(T_w) = (1,1) entry of the product of rho(Y) = [[1,0],[t^-3,t^-3]], rho(X) = [[1,1-t^4],[0,-t]] and inverses (checked by the lens on 130 tangles; by Claude by hand on 3_1 and 4_1).
 - THEOREM B: for every side-preserving strong inversion of a non-trivial two-bridge knot J is not a unit; extreme coefficients +-1. Proof by a no-tie (ping-pong) argument on the leading exponent of v_2/v_1 along the normal form in E = rho(Y)^-1 rho(X) and powers of Y. This is LEMMA Q FOR THE CLASS (all strong inversions of K(p/q) unless q^2 = 1 mod p). THEOREM D: T(p,p+1).
 - B is the unreduced complex of the trivial tangle written as two band crossings and their inverses with the Reidemeister II differential deleted; the offset (h,q) = (1,0) is the degree of that differential.
 - NEGATIVE (examples): longer specials exist: T(4,5): r + 2 s4 + s8 (move P_4); T(5,6): a rectangle component with sides P_4, D; T(6,7): s12. SHAPE and the list of hull directions of X.15 are FALSE beyond the table (directions nD + P occur). NP holds in all of them.
 - DATA: det K = det K_u det K_l; dim N = max(rank HFK^(K), rank Kh~(K_u) rank Kh~(K_l)) in all 132 table cases (9_46 second inversion: 15 = 5 x 3, constituents T(3,4), 3_1).
 - Proposed mechanism: J is a matrix coefficient of the mixed braid group B_{1,n} on Hom(V_1, V_1 (x) V_2^{(x) n}); Lemma Q = it is a unit only for the trivial theta-curve; route: tropical no-tie argument for reduced words (proved for n = 2).
**COLORED lens** (mech2/colored/REPORT.md). THEOREM A' (pointed Batson-Seed): spectral sequence E_1 = N(T) => Kh~(K_u) (x) Kh~(K_l), d_k of bidegree (1-2k,-2k), raising l = h - q by one; dim N >= rank Kh~(K_u) rank Kh~(K_l). THEOREM B': END and NP for doubles of adequate knots. Double of 8_19: dim N = 41 against 25 (neither sequence collapses). DATA: double extremality (top quantum degree has minimal delta and minimal l; dually) in 278/278.
**STATES lens** (mech2/states/REPORT.md). Band-diagram cube: extreme part is a Boolean cube; extreme degree F iff no bad block. Unit position U = (2c - 2wr, 2c - 4wr). THEOREM: a half-diagram with all crossings of one sign gives a non-flat tangle (Lemma Q for positive or negative half-diagrams). 72 of 138 table entries non-flat by diagram counts.
**ANNULAR lens** (mech2/annular/REPORT.md). SHAPE for unit arrows PROVED from the Kotelskiy-Watson-Zibrowius extension property (arXiv:2202.01460 Thm 4.4; mu_4 over B^*[U]). ADDITIVITY LEMMA (negative): every ingredient of the annular package is additive in the type D structure, so nothing of that kind excludes B or a ladder.

**PARALLELOGRAM LAW (Claude; DATA, 286 of 286 distinct tangles incl. the three Alexander-polynomial-one knots; tools/parallelogram.py).** In the (h,q) plane N(T) lies in the parallelogram with sides of slopes 1 and 2 whose opposite vertices are its bottom and top generators. Equivalently, in the two diagonal gradings delta = q/2 - h and l = h - q there is a generator minimising both and one maximising both. It implies NP. Every cancelling structure known so far uses arrows in that cone (D, P_n; Daemi's (r, 2r-2), slope 1 at r = 2; reversed Batson-Seed (2k-1, 2k)); the excluded offset (1,0) is outside it; slope 1 is the l-preserving direction (2,2) = P - 3D, which explains the old TILT bound 3.
**Side finding (to be refereed).** Grigsby-Wehrli Conjecture 7.1 (delta on the last page lifts the relative Maslov grading) is contradicted by the collapsed case K = 4_1, n = 2: delta levels (2,6,9,6,2) against Alexander ranks (1,6,11,6,1). The rows of N carry no Maslov or Alexander meaning.

## X.22. Two-bridge PROVED; the counterexample family is a two-channel twist family; next class (Claude, 16 November, 12:07; letter 63, queued during Caleb's pause)

**TH-2BR-A/B: PROVED.** Codex112801's skein proof (single f_2 projector slid to both band legs; H = Hom(V_1, V_1 (x) V_2 (x) V_2), dim 2; E_1 an X-eigenvector, phi a left Y-eigenvector; (YX^-1)^2 = Delta^2 scalar, YX^-1 nonscalar, so xi eta = t^-3(1-t^4)) read and accepted by Claude. Independent support: a finite stable-span proof of the matrix formula for every word (Claude referee, mech2/verify/twobridge-compute.md section 2); U(w) <=> det K_w = 1 <=> w in <X><Y> mod centre on all 3,188,645 freely reduced words of length <= 13; the prefix-normalized span formula exact on 46,810 normal forms (Claude, today) and on all words up to length 13 (referee). Corollary: J != 1 and J = 1 mod delta^2 give V(T(1/n)) != 1 for every n != 0. Endpoint laws: Claude's PARALLELOGRAM LAW (X.19) WITHDRAWN; the Floer report's "integrally Z" sentence WITHDRAWN.

**TWIST-2CH (DATA, exact; skein proof OPEN).** For T_n = quotient of s.r1.x2.(y1)^n.y0.r2.y0.y1.y0.y1.x2.u3.u1 (n = 1 is the endpoint counterexample), J(T_n) = alpha t^(2n) + beta (-t)^n with alpha = t^-5(t^2 - t + 1), beta = t^2(t^4 - t^3 - 1) + t^-6(t^2 - t + 1) (bracket state sum, u = 1, n = -12..24). Only the V_0 and V_2 channels of the y1 site reach the axis; eigenvalue ratio -t as for rho(X). No member is flat. In the same units the top of N is 2n + 6 for n = 1, 3, 5, 7, 9 (dim N = 33, 69, 105, 141, 177) and the bottom of N equals the bottom of J; the number of top slices with zero Euler characteristic is n for n <= 8 and 11 at n = 9 (both confirmed), 9 at n = 10 (confirmed, dim N = 195), predicted 9 for every n >= 10 (n = 11 run pending). So the outline failure is bounded in this family, and J != 1 is explained by two distinct channel eigenvalues, not by N's outline. Proposed general lemma: at any twist site of two arc strands, J(T_n) = sum over c in {0,2,4} of alpha_c lambda_c^n; a twist family has either one channel (J(T_n) = unit x J(T_0)) or finitely many flat members.

**NEXT CLASS (proposal).** Every sphere meeting the theta-curve in two axis points and two arc points carries a two-dimensional state space Hom(V_1 (x) V_2, V_1 (x) V_2); upstairs it is an h-invariant Conway sphere of kappa. Theorem B is Lemma Q for one such sphere with trivial outside. Next: kappa with an h-invariant Conway decomposition into rational pieces, first Montesinos knots with the horizontal-axis inversion (a strong inversion exactly when the axis meets kappa twice; model P(-2,3,2m+1)): J = F(v_1, ..., v_k) with each v_i a two-bridge-type vector and F a fixed frame tensor. Needed: marked frame, actual F, and a no-tie statement for F on vectors in the automaton's ratio states. Hyperbolic in general, so not covered by U5-SF or CX-GRAPH. P(-3,5,7) is non-invertible (Trotter) and is not a target.

Second-order refinement R_c,S_j gives potential rho(a)+[stateR] nonincreasing, so rho(a)<=-1 onR,<=0 onS and rho(b)<=0. If r boxes are R-top and d=2r-3N, cancelling top ties are (mu>=1,d0,r even), (mu<=-2,d-1,r odd), (mu-1,d-3,r even); mirror sends(r,d)to(n-r,-n-d), making simultaneous top/bottom cancellation impossible. No-tie cases have span>=4n-2; stronger uniform span/cascade bounds are not needed or banked.

## X.24. Class-free moment theory at minus one (Codex212109, 16 November)

Caleb's redirect in Claude210856 parks class-ladder extensions and targets the a2=0 core. Reply `collab/msgs/20261116-212109-codex.md`; proofs/referees `work/theory_restart/ft_integral_referee.md`, `ft_second_referee.md`, `roots_odd_referee.md`, `osp_moments_referee.md`. Only fixed local matrix identities and the two published figure-eight inversions were computed; no scan.

**FT-MINUS1, TRUSTED.** For m disjoint specified equivariant crossing changes, Delta^m J is divisible by (t+1)^m in Z[t,t^-1]. Thus M_k=k![h^k]J(-exp(h)) has equivariant finite-type order <=k. The specified moves are a symmetric off-axis pair (one color2-color2 crossing change in the quotient) and the full color1-color2 twist versus its inverse. An arbitrary single mixed half-crossing is outside the statement.

The written proof uses q=i exp(h/2), t=q², and the ramified self-dual lattice s²=[2]=q+q^-1. Color2 has weights2,0,-2 with E,F adjacent coefficients s. Its cups/caps are regular; the fusion inclusion i:V1->V1 tensor V2 and retraction p have pi=Id and only denominator [3], which is -1 at the root. Their explicit matrices and intertwining identities are in the proof/certificate. In the finite universal-R formula, B22's nonconstant terms contain s² and s4/[2]!=s², so B22 is unramified and reduces to the superflip on1|2. Therefore B22-B22^-1 is divisible by h, not merely s. Mixed braidings are regular over the ramified ring, and T=B21 B12 satisfies (T-q^-4)(T-q²)=0, hence T-T^-1=(t+1)[T-(t-1+t^-1)I], also h-divisible. Generic eigenvalue coalescence alone would not establish either lattice assertion.

Cutting the unknotted axis gives a regular scalar endomorphism of V1; no division by its vanishing specialized quantum dimension is used. The connected color2 arc is forced to the middle state by both vertices at the root, so the raw normalized scalar F has F0=1. Its generic identification with J is the banked theta skein calculation: f2 kills the turnback term, the surviving y term contributes the color2 circle [3], and dividing by the trivial theta value gives J up to a ribbon monomial. The singular projector is used only generically for identification, never to infer root regularity. Cube multilinearity supplies h^m, and s->-s gauge conjugation makes the scalar unramified. Writing F=1+f1 h+..., the preferred correction is J=exp(-h f1)F. Since f1 has finite-type order1 and products add order, the kth coefficient retains order<=k. Monic divisibility gives the integral Laurent statement. Matrix certificate `referee_checks/ft_local.py`; independent root/local/normalization read in `ft_second_referee.md`.

**FT-AXIS, TRUSTED.** At the root T0=Id tensor parity, and ((T-T^-1)/h)|0=-T0-3I. The raw first-order difference on the forced middle state is-4. Preferred normalization therefore changes the principal insertion to3(T0-I), zero on the middle state and-6 on the odd span. With all marked sites on-axis, the remaining root tensors preserve the middle label along the one connected arc; the principal contraction vanishes. Unramifiedness upgrades the apparent extra s to a whole h. Thus Delta_axis^m J lies in(t+1)^(m+1), m>=1. This is restricted all-axis leading-symbol vanishing, not an unrestricted1T claim. Off-axis principal insertion sends w1w1 to -2w0w2+2w2w0 in the displayed convention; it can create odd states and does not commute with the parity projector on one factor.

**Root superstructure, scope.** The superflip and parity monodromy are actual matrices, not eigenvalue-count heuristics. They give explicit local tensors for equivariant weight symbols. The reduced axis evaluation resolves the zero-quantum-dimension issue without an assumed modified trace. Full identification with an osp covering/defect category remains OPEN. Clark arXiv1509.03533 Thm3.8/Cor3.9 supplies a generic multicolored tangle functor, while Thm4.24 is a parameter/phase relation for same-colored knots. Neither proves the mixed root correspondence. Ebert2210.09583 has a rank2 covering module; its parameter-one sector must not be conflated with the ordinary classical symmetric supercategory, where double braiding is identity.

**ROOTS, TRUSTED independent read.** For G=(J-1)/(t+1)², closures1,2 at i force G(i)=0; closure1 at zeta6 forces G(zeta6)=0 because the alternative has quadratic norm1/3; closure1 at1 gives G(1)=0. Preferred integrality and Gauss's lemma yield D=(t+1)²(t-1)(t²+1)(t²-t+1)=(t4-1)(t3+1) dividing J-1. An extra t+1 follows when a2=0. Lickorish–Millett1986 Thms1,3 are the special-value input.

**ODD-CLOSURE-REPAIR, TRUSTED.** Every L_n=T(1/n) has determinant1; its symmetrized Seifert form is even unimodular, so sigma lies in8Z. Since L_(n+2) differs by one crossing change, the signatures agree on each parity class:0 even,-8j odd. With the displayed closure formula, J''(-1)=-24a2 and Mullins lambda=-V'(-1)/(12V(-1))+sigma/8, the matching cover orientation has lambda=-n a2. A Jones-unit odd closure must satisfy j=n a2 (sign reversed if the marking/cover orientation is reversed consistently). Thus a2|j with odd nonzero quotient is necessary, and at most one odd exception exists when a2!=0. On a2=0 core, Jones-unitness forces j0 and then J1; conversely J1 forces j0 by V'(1)=0. Hence V(L_n)=1 iff J1 for every n!=0 oncore. The earlier formal J* counterexample still refutes the algebra-only inference; the topology supplies the repair. Lemma Q for all inversions implies Jones detection for unknotting-one knots: Montesinos p/2 surgery has |p|1 once determinant1 is imposed.

**MOMENT-3, TRUSTED.** H=(J-1)/D is integral and H(-1)=a2. Differentiating D gives M3=36(2H'(-1)-7a2). Thus36|M3 universally and72|M3 oncore. The residual proposed iota integrality is exactly the unproved mod5 relation 2H'(-1)-7a2-7v3=0 mod5. As a rational invariant iota has degree<=3 and is primitive under equivariant sum. Fixed published figure-eight inversions s.l1.y0.y0.x1.u2 and s.l1.x0.x0.y1.u2 give J1=t^-6+t^-3-t^-2+1-t and J2=J1(t^-1), M3=-180,+180, v3=0, hence iota=-1,+1. This certifies actual inversion dependence; `referee_checks/figure_eight_inversion_moments.py/.json` records both inputs and outputs.

**Reduction and next crux.** The Taylor tower already determines J even without FT. The new theorem puts it in a controlled equivariant crossing filtration and removes the singular-root obstruction to its local weight tensors. Multiplicativity under sum makes log J a primitive finite-type series, order<=degree; oncore its first possible term is12H'(-1)h³. No separation theorem, finite cutoff or positivity has been proved. Next bounded target: identify the order3 principal weight and prove/refute the mod5 relation by a diagrammatic tensor comparison, not more sampled values or a class extension.

## X.25. Exact osp symbol and actual unbounded moment depth (Codex213301, 16 November)

Claude212613 independently confirms the complete FT/axis proof, promoting FT-MINUS1 and FT-AXIS to PROVED; ROOTS, the signature repair and M3 arithmetic have his read as well. Known evaluation hardness supplies no such reduction. Fixed-order Jones jets are polynomial-time computable and polynomially bounded in crossing count (Bar-Natan Theorems 1–2). Hence bounded-jet detectable output families have JT in P, and no polynomial-size knot encoding V-1=D(t)#SAT with fixed nonzero integral D exists. This is a restriction on exact gadget theory, not hardness or detection.

**BRIDGE-CONNECT / WIDTH, TRUSTED with independent read.** Full relative bracket equality up to +/-A^k preserves endpoint matching and closed-component count: close both tangles by each matching; evaluate absolute brackets at A=1; two component equations force the matchings to coincide. Fixed external substitutions cannot change component count under that equality. Seam cuts inside the content and scalar-only equalities are outside the claim. A supplied planar sweep of frontier width w computes the full polynomial in poly(N)exp(O(w)); uniformly logarithmic-width hardness gadgets would imply P=NP. The proposed imported tool is Holant/exact signature classification, retaining connectivity and formal Laurent coefficients; existing numerical dichotomies do not settle this problem.

**BRIDGE-REALIZE, OPEN mechanism.** KWZ's actual-tangle U-extension is a concrete derived-deformation input beyond K0, but existence of lifts is closed under direct sums and shifts (TRUSTED elementary block-diagonal lemma). If the compact BN block Q and arc lift, every formal ladder lifts. First bounded test: Q in the exact KWZ deformation, not a scan or wholesale new invariant. A stronger mechanism must exhibit a coherent geometric relation the ladder fails. Same bracket class never by itself controls stability mass; generic positivity remains parked.

**BRIDGE-ARITH, TRUSTED translation, OPEN leverage.** P is a Laurent unit iff every cyclotomic value is an algebraic-integer unit iff P has no nonzero root in any finite field. For normalized knot V, a unit is 1, so JC is equivalent to existence of such a zero for every nontrivial knot. No geometric construction of that zero is known here. Existing formal Habiro sequences separate color-two flatness from higher colors even with integrality/q-holonomicity; complete-colored detection is not an available solved endpoint. Source details and quantifiers are in the notes.

Recommended first bridge: exact counting-gadget theory under the proved depth, width and connectivity restrictions. The category test is a cheap filter for whether a proposed new theory retains a missing relation. All global cores remain OPEN; no outside communication or search resumed.

**BRIDGE-DEPTH, TRUSTED.** For an n-crossing knot, n>=1, the oriented skein relation telescoped to descending unlinks gives abs(V_K(exp(h)))<2 for complex abs(h)<=1/(16n). At smoothing depth s there are at most n^s unlink terms, at most s+1 components, s factors2sinh(h/2), and monomial exponent bounded by(2n+1)(s+1). The geometric-series bound is exp(4nR)/(1-2nR exp(4nR)); take R=1/(16n). Cauchy gives abs([h^r]V_K(exp(h)))<=2(16n)^r uniformly in r. This supplies the control of the order-dependent constant missing from an application of Bar-Natan's fixed-invariant Theorem2.

For nonzero V-1=N D, N integer and D an integer Laurent polynomial, write D=(t-1)^r E with E(1) a nonzero integer. The first exponential Taylor coefficient is N E(1), with no factorial denominator loss. Hence abs(N)<=2(16n)^r. Outputs abs(N)>=2^(alpha m) and n<=C m^d require r=Omega(m/log m). Mere N<=2^m does not. Integrality and the value-preserving factorization matter; this does not restrict every zero-test reduction. **FIXED-TWIST-P, TRUSTED corollary.** D-MULTITWIST already proves the finite-union affine fibre statement for a fixed marked finite twist template. Clear a fixed denominator and expand into a fixed number of monomials with integer affine exponents in the twist parameters. Balanced partitions give exact fibres; adding an unknown unit exponent and projecting gives affine lattice cosets, including parity classes. Their monomial exponent can vary. Brownawell-Masser is unnecessary. Sparse exponent grouping gives a polynomial binary-input membership test; an unknown unit exponent is determined by the least surviving numerator exponent minus that of the fixed denominator. Fix the entire outside template and channel system, not merely the slot count. Growing templates and arbitrary braid powers are outside the statement. This is an elementary tractable restriction, not topology of unit families or an unconditional ban on counting.

**BRIDGE-BAND, TRUSTED scoped translation.** With raw bracket[T]=y b+x c and unknot D(T), <N(T)>=(delta²-1)y+u=u[(delta²-1)J+1] in preferred framing. A coherent band gives common orientations and writhe; the two Jones equalities eliminate x and force y=u. Property R distinguishes the nonrational case from an actual unlink closure. Flat J1 forces j0 using V'(1)=0, so every nonzero knot closure is Jones-unit; their nontriviality uses the nontrivial-surgery theorem. The old odd-closure exception concerns J!=1, not this implication. No unknotted-component or crossing-circle necessity follows.

**GAUGE-SCOPE.** The gauge lens's chi(I^natural(K))=sigma(K)/2 is REFUTED by I^natural(U)=Z (Kronheimer-Mrowka section1.1). Its claim that all SU2 Euler information is exhausted by signature/determinant/Casson data is unsupported. ## X.38. Active JT2: constant braid gates and the topology of product carriers (17 November)

Caleb chose link complexity (Claude131345). Reply132522 adopts the split: Codex exact gadget algebra, Claude literature/actual constructions/referees. Three bounded internal algebra/source reads; no gate enumeration. Notes jt2_clause_gate_scope.md, jt2_difference_algebra.md, jt2_root_gates.md. All new deductions TRUSTED pending Claude. General exact JT2 hardness/realizability remains OPEN.

**CONSTANT-BRAID.** In a standard generic finite TL representation on N physical strands, let a braid subgroup Gamma preserve a generic code W and act in one common basis by scalar functions times constant complex matrices. Its constant projective image factors the permutation image of Gamma in S_N. At A1, each crossing squares toI, hence a pure braid isI on the full specialized module. The restriction spectrum u(A) times the constant spectrum must tend to1, so all constant eigenvalues agree. At a different regular sufficiently high-level unitary specialization, the code inherits a positive form, making the restriction diagonalizable. Thus each pure braid acts projectively trivially. A code basis need not extend regularly toA1. If the constant gate group contains A_(2^m), factorial orders force N>=2^m for m>=2. This is not a restriction on arbitrary A-dependent, changing-code, postselected, noninvertible or direct scalar-fibre gadgets.

**RATIONAL-HADAMARD.** Standard braid matrices and their inverses preserve the integral Laurent lattice. At the DVR(2), every eigenvalue therefore has valuation0. A restriction rationally conjugate to lambda H, H=[[1,1],[1,-1]], would have eigenvalues +/-lambda sqrt2 with valuation v2(lambda)+1/2, impossible for lambda inQ(A). Same for H tensorI. This is a separate arithmetic obstruction, with the rational encoding hypothesis retained; not a universal Boolean-circuit prohibition.

**CARRIER criterion.** For independent inputs, C(P_i+z_i D_i)=sum_E h_E product_(i in E)z_i. A baseline plus only the complete product requires h_E0 for every nonempty properE. The same criterion holds if the exact identity is prescribed on the entire Boolean cube. A mixed finite difference alone gives a signed sum of2^m diagrams, not one actual diagram. Pure vanishing relative vectors cannot be actual tangles since every actual closure has nonzeroA1 bracket.

The local weighted crossing difference Rplus-A²Rminus=A^-1(1-A⁴)e illustrates the bare-smoothing obstruction: each divided mixed corner is a scalar times an actual link bracket, neverzero. Arbitrary outside wiring cannot annihilate all proper subsets for these inputs. EKT divided defects may be signed combinations, so the exclusion does not apply to them without checking that hypothesis. Units and common writhe normalization are retained.

**Serial TL2.** With ell(I)=alpha!=0, ell(e)=beta, Z=beta I-alpha e, ell(Z²)=alpha beta(alpha delta-beta). This gives a formal scalar binaryAND, but open output retains(x+y)Z. For m>=3, singleton vanishing puts every defect onKZ; pair vanishing and the quadratic relation force every higher ell(Z^k)zero, contradicting a nonzero full product. Invertible backgrounds reduce to this case in the commutative algebra. It is only the fixed two-state serial architecture.

**PROJECTOR-COST.** Formal orthogonal projectors p0=e/delta,p1=I-p0 satisfy product(p0+z_i p1)=p0+product(z_i)p1. Integral carriers C(z)=e+z(delta I-e) satisfy product C_i=delta^(m-1) C(product z_i). If each is an actual tangle up to a monomial unit with z_i(1)=0, specialized bracket is+/-e. The boundary-matching proof already works from equality atA1 alone; each tangle has cup-cap matching and no closed components. Serial composition creates m-1 internal closed components, and every exterior closure adds at leastone, giving >=m total. They need not be split. Thus m>=3 cannot give JT2 in this literal architecture. Extra boundary legs or cuts inside carriers change the problem and remain open.

Formal matrix control: in Mat_(m+1), ordered M_i=I+z_i E_(i,i+1) and output M11+M1,m+1 give1+productz_i. Generic TL has growing matrix blocks, but rational matrix units and this output functional are not actual gates by declaration. The nontrivial unipotent M_i cannot be generic braid restrictions by the same positive-unitarity argument. This distinguishes algebraic capacity from geometric compilation.

**ROOT-GATES.** Use F_L(t)=t^-1/2 V_L(t)+1+t^-1, integral for two components. Specialized skein gives(t³-1)|F. Lickorish-Millett at i gives F(i) in{0,1-i,2(1-i)}, so integral F=N D with abs(N)>2 implies Phi4|D. If one D realizes counts1,2,3, at zeta6 the values -sqrt3+jC must belong to the signed real/imaginary powers in their Theorem3. They force C real; the signed-power arithmetic progression admits only C0 through j3. Hence Phi6|D. Thus a common prefactor(1-t)^m C needs fixed Phi3Phi4Phi6 factors under those count hypotheses. These gates can be met formally and do not construct links. F'(1)=-3lk, so depth>=2 also forces linking0. No universal determinant factor or knot-specific J-root claim is made.

Program corrections retained: fixed jets for links are inP; quantitative q-defect bound is8(32n)^r. Unbounded NO-image depth, superlogarithmic supplied width and escape from fixed-template tractability are conditional hardness barriers assuming P!=NP. Integral exponential-count amplitude bounds are unconditional.
